澳大利亚 慢性乙型肝炎 护理和 治疗 依从性的纵向模式:国家关联数据 队列研究 研究。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.
作者： Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
摘要中文翻译目前澳大利亚对 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗 的吸收和护理远低于目标水平,导致可预防的肝病和癌症的持续负担。了解个人层面的护理参与轨迹和社会人口因素的变化对于指导改善护理获取和保留的努力至关重要。这项研究分析了通过澳大利亚国家健康保险系统提供的乙型肝炎病毒载量 (VL) 检测和 Z​​XQTERMNQXZ 治疗 记录,这些记录与来自各个政府数据集的移民、死亡和社会人口统计数据相关。对人群的 治疗 和 VL 测试进行了评估,并使用慢性乙型肝炎患者人数的模型估计来计算吸收率。还评估了基于分发脚本的 VL 测试频率和 治疗 遵守情况。2023 年澳大利亚估计有 219,800 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,其中 118,334 人 (53.8%) 在 2014-2023 年期间接受了 VL 检测或 治疗。原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民对 VL 测试和 治疗 的估计采用率较低,并且因出生地区而异。在接受任何 VL 测试的 81,360 人中,只有 17,352 人 (21.3%) 定期接受测试,而 31,715 人 (39.0%) 有先前监测的证据,但自 2019 年以来没有进行 VL 测试。大多数开始 治疗 的人继续治疗并获得足够的供应以维持依从性。这项研究表明,澳大利亚在慢性乙型肝炎护理方面存在巨大差距,特别是在对未参加 治疗 的人群进行持续监测方面。优先人群的吸收差异很明显。应优先考虑重新参与那些失去随访的人。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗 和澳大利亚的护理利用率仍低于目标,导致可预防的肝病和癌症,凸显了了解个人护理轨迹和社会人口差异的必要性。
设计与对象
这项国家关联数据 队列研究 研究分析了澳大利亚国家健康保险系统中的乙型肝炎病毒载量检测记录和 抗病毒药物 治疗 记录,与移民、死亡和社会人口统计数据集相关联。
核心方法
使用慢性乙型肝炎患者人数的模型估计来评估 治疗 和病毒载量测试的采用情况,并根据分发的脚本评估测试频率和 治疗 依从性。
主要结果
2023 年,澳大利亚估计有 219,800 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,其中 118,334 人 (53.8%) 在 2014 年至 2023 年期间接受了病毒载量检测或 治疗,其中只有 21.3% 的接受定期监测。
解释与创新
该研究确定了慢性乙型肝炎护理方面的巨大差距,特别是在非 治疗 人群的持续监测方面,并强调了原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民等重点人群和出生地区之间的差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要重新吸引失访的个体,并优先考虑公平地获得病毒载量监测和 治疗,以减少可预防的肝病和癌症负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于行政数据,这可能低估了真正的护理参与度,并且缺乏详细的临床或病毒学结果,限制了因果推断和对更广泛人群的普遍性。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.

Authors: Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
Original abstract (English)
Current uptake of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care in Australia is well below target levels, leading to an ongoing burden of preventable liver disease and cancer. Understanding individual-level trajectories of care engagement and variation by sociodemographic factors is essential to guide efforts to improve access to and retention in care. This study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load (VL) testing and antiviral treatment provided through Australia's national health insurance system, which were linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic data from various government datasets. Treatment and VL testing by population group were assessed, and uptake was calculated using modelled estimates of the number living with CHB. VL testing frequency and treatment adherence based on scripts dispensed were also assessed. Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either VL testing or treatment during 2014-2023. Estimated uptake of VL testing and treatment uptake were lower among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and also varied by region of birth. Of the 81,360 who received any VL testing, only 17,352 (21.3%) received it regularly, while 31,715 (39.0%) had evidence of prior monitoring but no VL testing since 2019. The majority of those who initiated treatment continued therapy and received supply sufficient for adherence. This study shows that substantial gaps exist in CHB care in Australia, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment. Disparities in uptake are evident by priority population. Re-engaging those lost to follow-up should be prioritised.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care uptake in Australia remains below targets, contributing to preventable liver disease and cancer, highlighting a need to understand individual-level care trajectories and sociodemographic disparities.
Design and population
This national linked data cohort study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load testing and antiviral treatment from Australia's national health insurance system, linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic datasets.
Core methods
Treatment and viral load testing uptake were assessed using modelled estimates of the number of people living with CHB, with frequency of testing and treatment adherence evaluated based on scripts dispensed.
Main results
Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either viral load testing or treatment during 2014–2023, with only 21.3% of those tested receiving regular monitoring.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identifies substantial gaps in CHB care, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment, and highlights disparities by priority populations such as Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and region of birth.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need to re-engage individuals lost to follow-up and prioritise equitable access to viral load monitoring and treatment to reduce preventable liver disease and cancer burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on administrative data, which may underestimate true care engagement and lacks detailed clinical or virological outcomes, limiting causal inference and generalisability to broader populations.

未经治疗的免疫耐受者与免疫耐受者之间的肝细胞癌风险接受治疗的免疫活跃 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。

Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Between Untreated Immune Tolerant Vs. Treated Immune Active Chronic Hepatitis B Patients.
作者： Robert J Wong, Zeyuan Yang, Ramsey Cheung
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者在未经治疗的免疫耐受 (U-IT) 与治疗的免疫活跃 (T-IA) 阶段发生肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的风险尚不清楚,并且大多数数据来自亚洲人群。
设计与对象
我们对 2010 年 1 月 1 日至 2021 年 12 月 31 日期间符合 U-IT 或 T-IA 标准的慢性乙型肝炎退伍军人进行了倾向评分加权分析(随访至 2024 年 12 月 31 日)。总共确定了 591 名 U-IT 和 555 名 T-IA CHB 患者。
核心方法
比较 U-IT 组和 T-IA 组之间肝硬化、HCC 或死亡的 发病率。
主要结果
Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients.
解释与创新
在主要为非亚裔的慢性乙型肝炎成人 队列研究 中,我们观察到与 T-IA 患者相比,U-IT 患者的 HCC 风险较低。虽然这与亚洲国家的一些研究形成鲜明对比,但这些数据确实强调了在 IA 阶段优先考虑并确保及时治疗 CHB 患者的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在免疫活跃期对慢性乙型肝炎患者进行及时治疗的必要性,尽管免疫耐受患者的常规 治疗 仍存在争议,并且需要更多数据来更好地了解长期结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究主要是在退伍军人中非亚裔 队列研究 中进行的,这可能会限制对其他人群的推广。尽管有倾向评分加权,但由于观察设计和潜在的残留混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Between Untreated Immune Tolerant Vs. Treated Immune Active Chronic Hepatitis B Patients.

Authors: Robert J Wong, Zeyuan Yang, Ramsey Cheung
Original abstract (English)
The risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) between chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in the untreated immune tolerant (U-IT) vs. treated immune active (T-IA) phases is not clear, and most data are in Asian populations. We aim to evaluate long-term risks of cirrhosis, HCC, and overall mortality between U-IT vs. T-IA CHB patients among a national cohort of U.S. We conducted a propensity score weighted analysis of Veterans with CHB who met criteria for U-IT or T-IA from 1/1/2010 to 12/31/2021 (with follow-up through 12/31/2024). Incidence of cirrhosis, HCC, or death were compared between U-IT and T-IA groups. A total of 591 U-IT and 555 T-IA CHB patients were identified (mean age 56.4-57.5 years, 45%-50% non-Hispanic white, 37%-40% African American, 9%-10% Asian, 25%-29% concurrent diabetes, 35%-39% with FIB-4 > 3.25). Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients. Among a predominantly non-Asian cohort of adults with CHB, we observed that HCC risk was lower in U-IT patients compared to T-IA patients. While this contrasts with some studies in Asian countries, these data do emphasize the importance of prioritizing and ensuring timely therapy for CHB patients in the IA phase. However, routine treatment in IT patients remains controversial and more data are needed to better understand long-term outcomes.

Question and background
The risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) between chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients in the untreated immune tolerant (U-IT) vs. treated immune active (T-IA) phases is not clear, and most data are in Asian populations.
Design and population
We conducted a propensity score weighted analysis of Veterans with CHB who met criteria for U-IT or T-IA from 1/1/2010 to 12/31/2021 (with follow-up through 12/31/2024). A total of 591 U-IT and 555 T-IA CHB patients were identified.
Core methods
Incidence of cirrhosis, HCC, or death were compared between U-IT and T-IA groups.
Main results
Over a median follow up of 6.4 years (IQR 3.0-10.4), incidence of HCC was significantly lower in U-IT patients vs. T-IA patients (0.28 vs. 0.54 per 100 person-years; HR 0.52, 95% CI 0.32-0.83, p = 0.007). However, the risk of cirrhosis or all-cause mortality was similar between both T-IA and U-IT CHB patients.
Interpretation and novelty
Among a predominantly non-Asian cohort of adults with CHB, we observed that HCC risk was lower in U-IT patients compared to T-IA patients. While this contrasts with some studies in Asian countries, these data do emphasize the importance of prioritizing and ensuring timely therapy for CHB patients in the IA phase.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for timely therapy for CHB patients in the immune active phase, though routine treatment in immune tolerant patients remains controversial and more data are needed to better understand long-term outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a predominantly non-Asian cohort of Veterans, which may limit generalizability to other populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and potential residual confounding despite propensity score weighting.

患有 1 型糖尿病的儿科患者乙型肝炎抗体反应减弱;重新接种协议的必要性:病例对照研究。

Diminished Hepatitis B Antibody Response in Pediatrics With Type 1 Diabetes; the Need for Revaccination Protocol: A Case-Control Study.
作者： Erfanehsadat Shahim, Rosemina Bahrololoom, Maryam Ataollahi, Marzieh Jamalidoust, Amirhossein Hasani, Homa Ilkhanipoor, Anahita Sanaei Dashti, Seyedeh Sedigheh Hamzavi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染是儿科患者中一种重要的慢性感染。本研究旨在评估和比较 1 型糖尿病 (T1DM) 儿童和非糖尿病参与者之间的血清保护作用。
设计与对象
在这项病例对照研究中,从设拉子大学儿科内分泌诊所招募了 127 名 T1DM 儿童和 124 名对照儿童,他们均处于健康的免疫状态,并完全接种了 HBV 疫苗。排除标准包括免疫抑制、疫苗接种 不完整或儿童/家庭有肝炎病史。
核心方法
获得设拉子医科大学的伦理批准。使用适当的推理测试对性别、年龄、抗体滴度和糖尿病指数进行比较;通过逻辑回归评估抗体滴度不足的几率。
主要结果
考虑到抗体滴度为 10 mIU/mL 就足够了,糖尿病患者的无保护率为 54.3%(95% CI:45.3%-63.2%),非糖尿病患者的无保护率为 28.2%(95% CI:20.5%-37.0%)。性别、血糖控制和病程超过1年没有显着影响。
解释与创新
与非糖尿病儿童相比,1 型糖尿病儿童表现出较低的抗 HBs 抗体滴度,且与性别或血糖控制没有关联。在儿童中评估 免疫应答 与 HBV 疫苗接种 时,应考虑 免疫应答 的时机和糖尿病状况。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要为 1 型糖尿病儿童制定重新接种疫苗方案,以确保充分预防 HBV。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖抗体滴度作为保护性免疫的标志,并且可能存在混杂因素。由于研究设计和病例对照方法的使用,证据强度适中。

Diminished Hepatitis B Antibody Response in Pediatrics With Type 1 Diabetes; the Need for Revaccination Protocol: A Case-Control Study.

Authors: Erfanehsadat Shahim, Rosemina Bahrololoom, Maryam Ataollahi, Marzieh Jamalidoust, Amirhossein Hasani, Homa Ilkhanipoor, Anahita Sanaei Dashti, Seyedeh Sedigheh Hamzavi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a significant chronic infection in pediatric patients. Vaccination induces production of anti-Hepatitis B surface antibodies, which serve as markers of protective immunity. Given the long-term risks of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma during adulthood, this study aimed to assess and compare seroprotection between children with type 1 diabetes mellitus (T1DM) and nondiabetic participants. METHODS: In this case-control study, 127 children with T1DM and 124 controls, all in a healthy immune state and fully vaccinated against HBV, were recruited from Shiraz University pediatric endocrinology clinics. Exclusion criteria included immunosuppression, incomplete vaccination, or hepatitis history in the child/family. Ethical approval was obtained from Shiraz University of Medical Sciences. Comparisons of sex, age, antibody titers, and diabetes indices used appropriate inferential tests; odds of inadequate antibody titers were assessed by logistic regression. RESULTS: Considering an antibody titer of 10 mIU/mL as adequate, the non-protection rate was 54.3% (95% CI: 45.3%-63.2%) in diabetic individuals and 28.2% (95% CI:20.5%-37.0%) in non-diabetics. In a multivariable model controlling for time from vaccination, time from vaccination (OR = 1.26, 95% CI: 1.06-1.49, p < 0.001), and diabetes status (OR = 2.34, 95% CI: 1.33-4.14, p = 0.002) contributed to inadequate antibody titers. Sex, glycemic control, and disease duration over 1 year had no significant effect. CONCLUSION: Children with type 1 Diabetes Mellitus exhibited lower anti-HBs antibody titers compared to their nondiabetic counterparts, with no association found with sex or glycemic control. The timing of vaccination and diabetes status should be considered when evaluating immune responses to HBV vaccination in children.

Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a significant chronic infection in pediatric patients. This study aimed to assess and compare seroprotection between children with type 1 diabetes mellitus (T1DM) and nondiabetic participants.
Design and population
In this case-control study, 127 children with T1DM and 124 controls, all in a healthy immune state and fully vaccinated against HBV, were recruited from Shiraz University pediatric endocrinology clinics. Exclusion criteria included immunosuppression, incomplete vaccination, or hepatitis history in the child/family.
Core methods
Ethical approval was obtained from Shiraz University of Medical Sciences. Comparisons of sex, age, antibody titers, and diabetes indices used appropriate inferential tests; odds of inadequate antibody titers were assessed by logistic regression.
Main results
Considering an antibody titer of 10 mIU/mL as adequate, the non-protection rate was 54.3% (95% CI: 45.3%-63.2%) in diabetic individuals and 28.2% (95% CI:20.5%-37.0%) in non-diabetics. Sex, glycemic control, and disease duration over 1 year had no significant effect.
Interpretation and novelty
Children with type 1 Diabetes Mellitus exhibited lower anti-HBs antibody titers compared to their nondiabetic counterparts, with no association found with sex or glycemic control. The timing of vaccination and diabetes status should be considered when evaluating immune responses to HBV vaccination in children.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for a revaccination protocol for children with type 1 diabetes mellitus to ensure adequate protection against HBV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on antibody titers as a marker of protective immunity and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the study design and the use of a case-control approach.

乌兹别克斯坦慢性丁型肝炎病毒感染患者的晚期慢性肝病发病率很高。

High Rates of Advanced Chronic Liver Disease in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Uzbekistan.

High Rates of Advanced Chronic Liver Disease in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Uzbekistan.

Authors: Malika E Khodjaeva, Aziza S Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz S Ibadullaeva, Gulnara K Khudaykulova, Munisa M Ilyasova, Kamila U Eshbayeva, Dildorakhon N Abdurakhmanova, Helenie Kefalakes, Petra Huber, Michael J Gebel
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is endemic in Uzbekistan. Previous data showed a high prevalence of anti-hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibodies in HBsAg-positive individuals. Limited data are available on disease severity, treatment availability and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prevalence in patients with chronic HDV infection in Uzbekistan. METHODS: The Scientific Research Institute of Virology (SRIoV) is the leading medical and scientific reference centre for viral hepatitis in Uzbekistan. In this retrospective study, epidemiological, clinical and laboratory data were collected and analysed from all HBsAg-positive patients presenting to the SRIoV between June 2023 and May 2024. Data from HDV-coinfected and HBV-monoinfected patients were compared. RESULTS: Data of 1393 individual patients were included. HDV coinfection was more prevalent than HBV-monoinfection (73.9% [n = 1030/1393]) vs. 26.1% [n = 363/1393]. HDV-infected patients were significantly younger (41.9 vs. 45.4, p upper limit of normal. The HCC detection rate was low in both patient groups due to insufficient screening measures (HDV: n = 7/1030, HBV: n = 11/363). CONCLUSIONS: In this retrospective cohort of 1393 individual patients, HDV coinfection exceeds HBV monoinfection in prevalence and disease severity. Most HDV-infected patients present with advanced liver disease and currently lack access to HDV-directed therapy.

Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is endemic in Uzbekistan, and previous data showed a high prevalence of anti-hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibodies in HBsAg-positive individuals, but limited data are available on disease severity, treatment availability and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prevalence in patients with chronic HDV infection in Uzbekistan.
Design and population
This retrospective study was conducted at the Scientific Research Institute of Virology (SRIoV), the leading medical and scientific reference centre for viral hepatitis in Uzbekistan, where epidemiological, clinical and laboratory data were collected and analysed from all HBsAg-positive patients presenting to the SRIoV between June 2023 and May 2024.
Core methods
Data from HDV-coinfected and HBV-monoinfected patients were compared, with a total of 1393 individual patients included in the analysis.
Main results
HDV coinfection was more prevalent than HBV-monoinfection (73.9% [n = 1030/1393] vs. 26.1% [n = 363/1393]), and HDV-infected patients were significantly younger (41.9 vs. 45.4, p < 0.001) but had more advanced liver disease, with higher rates of cirrhosis (41.3% vs. 25.3%, p < 0.001) and elevated ALT levels (58.6% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HDV coinfection exceeds HBV monoinfection in prevalence and disease severity, and most HDV-infected patients present with advanced liver disease and currently lack access to HDV-directed therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the urgent need for improved screening and treatment access for HDV-infected patients in Uzbekistan to address the high burden of advanced liver disease and reduce the risk of HCC.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the low HCC detection rate in both patient groups due to insufficient screening measures, but the large sample size and comprehensive data collection provide moderate evidence strength.

使用 AIE 编码的纳米生物探针进行乙型肝炎包膜抗原检测的高灵敏度和特异性荧光免疫分析。

A highly sensitive and specific fluorescent immunoassay for hepatitis B envelope antigen detection using AIE-encoded nanobioprobes.
作者： Yixin Shi, Xinxin Lu, Yifang Li, Youlang Cao, Ling-Hong Xiong, Xuewen He
摘要中文翻译开发适合基层医疗机构的高灵敏、高效、稳定、低成本的病毒抗原检测策略对于病毒感染的早期诊断和治疗具有重要意义。在此,我们报告了一种基于聚集诱导发射(AIE)分子编码荧光纳米球的高效荧光免疫分析策略,用于检测乙型肝炎包膜抗原(HBeAg)。通过超声溶胀法将三种AIE发光体(TPE-Br4、TPE-O2和TPE-O3)封装到羧基功能化的纳米球中,产生均匀且分散良好的多色荧光纳米球。其中,具有优异光稳定性的发红光纳米球被选为信号输出单元。随后将抗乙型肝炎包膜抗体缀合到其表面以构建荧光纳米生物探针。通过将这些纳米生物探针与夹心型酶联免疫吸附测定相结合,成功实现了 HBeAg 的特异性捕获和识别。该方法的线性检测范围为0.01-1 ng mL -1,超低检测限(LOD)为9 pg mL -1。此外,它在加标的新鲜小鼠血清样品中表现出优异的特异性和强大的抗干扰性能。总体而言,这项工作为 HBeAg 检测提供了一个灵敏、方便且经济高效的传感平台,在临床应用中显示出快速现场筛选病毒抗原的巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对适合初级医疗保健环境的高度灵敏、高效、稳定和低成本病毒抗原检测策略的需求,以实现病毒感染的早期 诊断 和 治疗。
设计与对象
该研究报告了使用聚集诱导发射(AIE)分子编码的荧光纳米球检测乙型肝炎包膜抗原(HBeAg)的荧光免疫分析策略的开发。
核心方法
通过超声波溶胀法将三种AIE发光体封装到羧基功能化的纳米球中,产生均匀且分散良好的多色荧光纳米球,其中红光纳米球因其优异的光稳定性而被选择。将抗乙型肝炎包膜抗体偶联到纳米球上,构建荧光纳米生物探针,将其与夹心型酶联免疫吸附测定结合,用于特异性捕获和识别 HBeAg。
主要结果
该方法表现出0.01-1 ng mL-1的线性检测范围和9 pg mL-1的超低检测限,在加标的新鲜小鼠血清样品中具有优异的特异性和强大的抗干扰性能。
解释与创新
作者提出了一种灵敏、方便且经济高效的 HBeAg 检测传感平台,强调了其在临床应用中快速现场筛查病毒抗原的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,该平台可以通过在初级医疗机构中实现快速且可访问的抗原检测来改善 诊断 和 治疗 感染的早期 诊断 和 治疗 感染。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据不报告临床验证、患者 队列研究 或现实世界的诊断性能,从而将证据的强度限制为基于实验室的概念验证。

A highly sensitive and specific fluorescent immunoassay for hepatitis B envelope antigen detection using AIE-encoded nanobioprobes.

Authors: Yixin Shi, Xinxin Lu, Yifang Li, Youlang Cao, Ling-Hong Xiong, Xuewen He
Original abstract (English)
Developing highly sensitive, efficient, stable, and low-cost viral antigen detection strategies suitable for primary healthcare settings is of great significance for the early diagnosis and treatment of viral infections. Herein, we report a highly efficient fluorescent immunoassay strategy for the detection of hepatitis B envelope antigen (HBeAg) based on aggregation-induced emission (AIE) molecule-encoded fluorescent nanospheres. Three AIE luminogens (TPE-Br4, TPE-O2, and TPE-O3) were encapsulated into carboxyl-functionalized nanospheres via an ultrasonic swelling method, yielding uniform and well-dispersed multicolor fluorescent nanospheres. Among them, the red-emitting nanospheres, exhibiting superior photostability, were selected as signal output units. Anti-hepatitis B envelope antibodies were subsequently conjugated onto their surfaces to construct fluorescent nanobioprobes. By integrating these nanobioprobes with a sandwich-type enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay, specific capture and recognition of HBeAg were successfully achieved. The proposed method demonstrated a linear detection range of 0.01-1 ng mL -1 and an ultra-low limit of detection (LOD) of 9 pg mL -1 . In addition, it exhibited excellent specificity and robust anti-interference performance in spiked fresh mouse serum samples. Overall, this work presents a sensitive, convenient, and cost-effective sensing platform for HBeAg detection, showing great potential for rapid and on-site screening of viral antigens in clinical applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for highly sensitive, efficient, stable, and low-cost viral antigen detection strategies suitable for primary healthcare settings to enable early diagnosis and treatment of viral infections.
Design and population
The study reports the development of a fluorescent immunoassay strategy for detecting hepatitis B envelope antigen (HBeAg) using aggregation-induced emission (AIE) molecule-encoded fluorescent nanospheres.
Core methods
Three AIE luminogens were encapsulated into carboxyl-functionalized nanospheres via an ultrasonic swelling method to produce uniform and well-dispersed multicolor fluorescent nanospheres, with red-emitting nanospheres selected for their superior photostability. Anti-hepatitis B envelope antibodies were conjugated onto the nanospheres to construct fluorescent nanobioprobes, which were integrated with a sandwich-type enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay for specific capture and recognition of HBeAg.
Main results
The proposed method demonstrated a linear detection range of 0.01-1 ng mL-1 and an ultra-low limit of detection of 9 pg mL-1, with excellent specificity and robust anti-interference performance in spiked fresh mouse serum samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors present a sensitive, convenient, and cost-effective sensing platform for HBeAg detection, highlighting its potential for rapid and on-site screening of viral antigens in clinical applications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this platform could improve early diagnosis and treatment of hepatitis B virus infections by enabling rapid and accessible antigen detection in primary healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report clinical validation, patient cohorts, or real-world diagnostic performance, limiting the strength of the evidence to a laboratory-based proof-of-concept.

慢性乙型肝炎 接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的 HIV 阴性患者的原发性腮腺内淋巴结卡波西肉瘤:罕见的 病例报告

Primary Intraparotid Lymph Node Kaposi Sarcoma in an HIV-negative Patient with Chronic Hepatitis B Receiving Antiviral Therapy: A Rare Case Report
作者： NIZAR MUHANNA, Majid Al-Abdulla, Lana Muhanna, Moosa Shibani, Maher Muhanna, Angie Hawat
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:卡波西肉瘤 (KS) 是一种与人疱疹病毒 8 (HHV-8) 相关的血管肿瘤,最常见于 HIV 感染或其他形式的免疫抑制患者。原发性腮腺内淋巴结受累极为罕见,特别是在 HIV 阴性个体中。我们报告了一名接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者发生孤立性腮腺内淋巴结 KS 的罕见病例。病例介绍:一名患有 慢性乙型肝炎 的 58 岁 HIV 阴性男性接受 抗病毒药物 治疗,出现左腮腺肿块逐渐增大,持续 3-4 个月。对比增强计算机断层扫描显示腮腺浅叶内有明确的增强病变。患者接受了腮腺浅表切除术,保留了面神经。组织病理学检查,在 HHV-8 免疫组织化学阳性的支持下,证实卡波西肉瘤起源于腮腺内淋巴结。两次独立进行的术后全身 PET/CT 未显示残留肿瘤或全身性疾病的证据。结论:该病例突出显示,在同时接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的 HIV 阴性患者中,卡波西肉瘤表现为孤立性腮腺内淋巴结病变,且同时接受 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗。它扩展了关于这种罕见实体的有限文献,并强调即使在没有 HIV 感染或播散性疾病的情况下,在非典型腮腺肿块的鉴别 诊断 中也应考虑卡波西肉瘤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究解决了 慢性乙型肝炎 HIV 阴性患者腮腺内淋巴结中罕见的卡波西肉瘤表现,突显了非 HIV 免疫抑制个体中非典型 KS 表现的知识差距。
设计与对象
一名 58 岁 HIV 阴性男性 病例报告 患者接受 抗病毒药物 治疗,出现左腮腺肿块逐渐增大。
核心方法
患者接受了保留面神经的腮腺浅表切除术,HHV-8免疫组织化学支持的组织病理学检查证实了诊断。
主要结果
增强CT显示腮腺内有边界清楚的增强病灶,术后PET/CT显示无残留肿瘤或全身性疾病。
解释与创新
作者将此解释为 HIV 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中异常罕见的孤立性腮腺内淋巴结 KS,扩大了有关该实体的有限文献。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调,即使没有 HIV 感染或播散性疾病,在非典型腮腺肿块的鉴别 诊断 中也应考虑卡波西肉瘤。
局限与证据强度
证据强度受到 病例报告 设计的限制,缺乏普遍性和对照组,但详细的组织病理学和影像学结果为 诊断 提供了适度的信心。

Primary Intraparotid Lymph Node Kaposi Sarcoma in an HIV-negative Patient with Chronic Hepatitis B Receiving Antiviral Therapy: A Rare Case Report

Authors: NIZAR MUHANNA, Majid Al-Abdulla, Lana Muhanna, Moosa Shibani, Maher Muhanna, Angie Hawat
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background : Kaposi sarcoma (KS) is a vascular neoplasm associated with human herpesvirus-8 (HHV-8) and is most commonly encountered in patients with HIV infection or other forms of immunosuppression. Primary involvement of an intraparotid lymph node is exceptionally rare, particularly in HIV-negative individuals. We report an unusual case of isolated intraparotid nodal KS in a patient with chronic hepatitis B receiving antiviral therapy. Case Presentation: A 58-year-old HIV-negative man with chronic hepatitis B receiving antiviral therapy presented with a gradually enlarging left parotid mass of 3–4 months’ duration. Contrast-enhanced computed tomography demonstrated a well-defined enhancing lesion within the superficial lobe of the parotid gland. The patient underwent superficial parotidectomy with preservation of the facial nerve. Histopathological examination, supported by positive HHV-8 immunohistochemistry, confirmed Kaposi sarcoma arising in an intraparotid lymph node. Postoperative whole-body PET/CT, performed on two separate occasions, showed no evidence of residual tumor or systemic disease. Conclusion: This case highlights an exceptionally rare presentation of Kaposi sarcoma as an isolated intraparotid lymph node lesion in an HIV-negative patient with concomitant chronic hepatitis B receiving antiviral therapy. It expands the limited literature on this uncommon entity and emphasizes that Kaposi sarcoma should be considered in the differential diagnosis of atypical parotid masses, even in the absence of HIV infection or disseminated disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study addresses the rare presentation of Kaposi sarcoma in an intraparotid lymph node of an HIV-negative patient with chronic hepatitis B, highlighting a knowledge gap in atypical KS manifestations in non-HIV immunosuppressed individuals.
Design and population
A case report of a 58-year-old HIV-negative man with chronic hepatitis B receiving antiviral therapy who presented with a gradually enlarging left parotid mass.
Core methods
The patient underwent superficial parotidectomy with facial nerve preservation, and histopathological examination supported by HHV-8 immunohistochemistry confirmed the diagnosis.
Main results
Contrast-enhanced CT showed a well-defined enhancing lesion in the parotid gland, and postoperative PET/CT revealed no residual tumor or systemic disease.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this as an exceptionally rare isolated intraparotid nodal KS in an HIV-negative patient with chronic hepatitis B, expanding the limited literature on this entity.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case emphasizes that Kaposi sarcoma should be considered in the differential diagnosis of atypical parotid masses, even in the absence of HIV infection or disseminated disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited by the case report design, which lacks generalizability and control groups, but the detailed histopathological and imaging findings provide moderate confidence in the diagnosis.

慢性肾病患者乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎感染的频率:巴基斯坦拉合尔的一项回顾性数据研究

Frequency of Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C Infections Among Patients With Chronic Kidney Disease: A Retrospective Data-Based Study From Lahore, Pakistan
作者： Muhammad Talha Sami, Ahtisham Liaqat, Muhammad Tayyab Latif, Naeem Akram, Aazia Sami, Nauman Ismat Butt
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 和慢性丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 感染是全球重大的公共卫生挑战,影响着全世界数百万人,并大大增加了慢性肝病及其并发症导致的发病率和 死亡率。本研究旨在分别确定 CKD 患者中 HBV 和 HCV 感染的频率,并评估其与巴基斯坦当地人口的人口和临床因素的关系。
设计与对象
这项基于数据的回顾性研究是在 Chaudhary Muhammad Akram 教学研究医院(拉合尔,PAK)进行的,纳入了 2025 年 1 月至 12 月到医学和肾脏病科进行透析和入住 ICU 的 317 份 18 岁以上 CKD 患者的医疗记录。
核心方法
使用结构化形式从患者病历中提取人口特征、CKD 相关变量和实验室参数,包括 HBsAg 和抗 HCV 抗体。使用 SPSS Statistics 版本 23(IBM Corp.,Armonk,NY,USA)分析数据。
主要结果
13 例 (4.1%) 检测出乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性,131 例 (41.3%) 检测出抗 HCV 阳性。五人 (1.5%) HBsAg 和抗 HCV 均呈阳性。卡方分析显示,抗-HCV 阳性与男性(p<0.001)、城市居住(p=0.038)、CKD 病程≥2 年(p=0.027)、晚期 CKD 分期(p<0.001)、心血管疾病(p=0.049)、输血史(p<0.001)和透析史(p<0.001)显着相关。
解释与创新
丙型肝炎病毒在 CKD 患者中非常流行;乙型肝炎病毒仍然相对不常见。这强调了对 CKD 患者进行常规筛查的必要性,特别是那些接受透析或接受输血的患者。
科研与公卫意义
加强感染控制措施、确保安全输血实践、推广 HBV 疫苗接种 并提供及时的 抗病毒药物 管理可能会减轻病毒性肝炎的负担并改善这一高危人群的临床结果。
局限与证据强度
由于 HBV 阳性的参与者数量有限,因此未对 HBsAg 进行回归分析。由于回顾性设计和未完全解决潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Frequency of Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C Infections Among Patients With Chronic Kidney Disease: A Retrospective Data-Based Study From Lahore, Pakistan

Authors: Muhammad Talha Sami, Ahtisham Liaqat, Muhammad Tayyab Latif, Naeem Akram, Aazia Sami, Nauman Ismat Butt
Original abstract (English)
Background and objective Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) and chronic hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections are significant global public health challenges, affecting millions of individuals worldwide and contributing substantially to morbidity and mortality due to chronic liver disease and its complications. In Pakistan, the burden of viral hepatitis remains considerable, with HBV and HCV infections continuing to affect vulnerable populations and placing an additional healthcare burden on patients with chronic diseases, including chronic kidney disease (CKD). This study aimed to determine the frequency of both HBV and HCV infections separately among CKD patients and to evaluate their relationship with demographic and clinical factors in a local Pakistani population. Methods This retrospective data-based study was conducted at Chaudhary Muhammad Akram Teaching and Research Hospital (Lahore, PAK) and included 317 medical records of CKD patients aged ≥18 years who presented to the Department of Medicine and Nephrology for dialysis and ICU admission from January to December 2025. Incomplete medical records, patients with acute kidney injury, and records missing hepatitis serologies were excluded. A structured pro forma was used to extract demographic characteristics, CKD-related variables, and laboratory parameters including HBsAg and anti-HCV antibodies from patient medical records. Data were analyzed using SPSS Statistics version 23 (IBM Corp., Armonk, NY, USA). Results Of the 317 CKD patients, 185 (58.4%) were male, and 184 (58.0%) resided in urban areas. Mean CKD duration was 2.9±2.8 years, with 185 (58.4%) having CKD for ≥2 years and 246 (77.6%) with stage 5 CKD. Hypertension was present in 271 (85.5%), diabetes in 171 (53.9%), and cardiovascular disease in 72 (22.7%). History of blood transfusion was reported in 140 (44.2%), while 214 (67.5%) had a history of dialysis. The mean hemoglobin (Hb) was 9.3±1.5 g/dL, with 247 (77.9%) having Hb>8 g/dL. Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) positivity was detected in 13 (4.1%) and anti-HCV in 131 (41.3%). Five (1.5%) had both HBsAg and anti-HCV positivity. Chi-square analysis revealed significantly associated anti-HCV positivity with the male gender (p<0.001), urban residence (p=0.038), CKD duration ≥2 years (p=0.027), advanced CKD stage (p<0.001), cardiovascular disease (p=0.049), blood transfusion history (p<0.001), and dialysis history (p<0.001). Binary logistic regression analysis demonstrated male gender (OR=0.483, 95% CI: 0.277-0.839; p=0.010), urban residence (OR=0.520, 95% CI: 0.305-0.887; p=0.016), positive history of blood transfusion (OR=0.348, 95% CI: 0.183-0.660; p=0.001), and dialysis history (OR=0.211, 95% CI: 0.091-0.488; p<0.001) to be predictors of anti-HCV positivity. Fisher's exact test demonstrated a significant association of HBsAg with the female gender (p=0.047) and diabetes (p<0.001). Regression analysis was not applied for HBsAg due to the limited number of participants with HBV positivity. Conclusion Hepatitis C virus was highly prevalent in CKD patients; HBV remained comparatively uncommon. This emphasizes the need for routine screening among CKD patients, especially those undergoing dialysis or receiving blood transfusions. Strengthening infection-control measures, ensuring safe transfusion practices, promoting HBV vaccination, and providing timely antiviral management may reduce the burden of viral hepatitis and improve clinical outcomes in this high-risk population.

Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) and chronic hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections are significant global public health challenges, affecting millions of individuals worldwide and contributing substantially to morbidity and mortality due to chronic liver disease and its complications. This study aimed to determine the frequency of both HBV and HCV infections separately among CKD patients and to evaluate their relationship with demographic and clinical factors in a local Pakistani population.

在未进行 抗病毒药物 预防的情况下接受全身治疗的 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阳性乳腺癌患者中临床检测到的 乙型肝炎病毒 再激活:两中心描述性 队列研究 和巢式病例研究

Clinically detected hepatitis B virus reactivation in HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-positive breast cancer patients receiving systemic therapy without antiviral prophylaxis: a two-centre descriptive cohort and nested case study
作者： Göknur Yapar Toros, Sabahat Çeken, Ayşe Ocak Duran, Öztürk Ateş
摘要中文翻译背景 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是接受细胞毒性癌症治疗的 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阳性患者的公认并发症。指南将接触蒽环类药物的抗 HBc 阳性患者置于中等风险范围,需要进行 治疗 监测或选择性预防,但来自非预防性乳腺癌实践的真实世界数据(其中连续 HBV DNA 监测 往往不完整)仍然很少。方法 我们回顾性研究了 2018 年至 2024 年在土耳其两个中心接受全身治疗的 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阳性乳腺癌患者,其中没有患者接受 抗病毒药物 预防。我们提取了人口统计学、肿瘤学和病毒学数据,包括基线抗 HBs 滴度以及所有可用的 HBV DNA 和丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 结果,并量化了病毒学 监测 的完整性。由于仅在临床怀疑有肝炎时才测试 治疗 HBV DNA,因此捕获的结果是临床检测到的再激活;因此,该研究描述了 发病率 的质量,而不是估计真实的 发病率。对于单个事件,分析保持描述性。结果 139 名患者(平均年龄 60.1 岁)中,89.2% 为抗 HBs 阳性,65.5% 接受蒽环类药物治疗。所有患者的基线 HBV DNA 均得到记录,但 治疗 上的连续监测并未记录。一位接受阿霉素-环磷酰胺治疗的抗 HBs 阴性女性发生了一次临床上明显的再激活(0.7%;95% CI 0.02%–3.94%),在第 4 个月表现为黄疸型肝炎,并对恩替卡韦有反应。由于亚临床事件可能会逃脱检测,因此该数字最好理解为下限,而不是真正的 发病率。结论 我们检测到的单次再激活出现在患者资料中,指南将其标记为中等风险——缺乏抗 HBs 以及蒽环类药物暴露。考虑到一个事件和不完整的 监测,这些观察结果与风险适应方法一致,但并未建立。需要使用标准化连续 HBV DNA 监测的前瞻性研究来估计该人群中真实的再激活 发病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是接受细胞毒性癌症治疗的 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阳性患者的公认并发症,但来自不完整连续 HBV DNA 监测 的非预防性乳腺癌实践的真实世界数据仍然很少。
设计与对象
我们回顾性研究了 2018 年至 2024 年间在土耳其两个中心接受全身治疗的 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阳性乳腺癌患者,其中没有人接受 抗病毒药物 预防。
核心方法
我们提取了人口统计学、肿瘤学和病毒学数据,包括基线抗 HBs 滴度以及所有可用的 HBV DNA 和丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 结果,并量化了病毒学 监测 的完整性。
主要结果
139 名患者(平均年龄 60.1 岁)中,89.2% 为抗 HBs 阳性,65.5% 接受蒽环类药物治疗;所有患者均记录了基线 HBV DNA,但未记录 治疗 连续监测。一位接受阿霉素-环磷酰胺治疗的抗 HBs 阴性女性发生了一次临床上明显的再激活(0.7%;95% CI 0.02%–3.94%),在第 4 个月表现为黄疸型肝炎,并对恩替卡韦有反应。
解释与创新
在患者资料中检测到的单次再激活被指南标记为中等风险——缺乏抗-HBs 以及蒽环类药物暴露。考虑到一个事件和不完整的 监测,这些观察结果与风险适应方法一致,但并未建立。
科研与公卫意义
需要使用标准化连续 HBV DNA 监测的前瞻性研究来估计该人群中真正的再激活 发病率,因为由于亚临床事件逃脱了检测,报告的数字最好解读为下限而不是真正的 发病率。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度受到单一事件和不完整的 发病率 的限制,这阻碍了对真实 发病率 的估计,并且可能错过了亚临床再激活,从而导致描述性分析而不是推理性分析。

Clinically detected hepatitis B virus reactivation in HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-positive breast cancer patients receiving systemic therapy without antiviral prophylaxis: a two-centre descriptive cohort and nested case study

Authors: Göknur Yapar Toros, Sabahat Çeken, Ayşe Ocak Duran, Öztürk Ateş
Original abstract (English)
Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a recognised complication in HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-positive patients receiving cytotoxic cancer therapy. Guidelines place anti-HBc-positive patients exposed to anthracyclines in a moderate-risk band warranting on-treatment monitoring or selective prophylaxis, yet real-world data from non-prophylaxis breast cancer practice, where serial HBV DNA surveillance is often incomplete, remain scarce. Methods We retrospectively studied HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-positive breast cancer patients treated with systemic therapy between 2018 and 2024 at two centres in Turkey, none of whom received antiviral prophylaxis. We extracted demographic, oncological and virological data, including baseline anti-HBs titres and all available HBV DNA and alanine aminotransferase (ALT) results, and we quantified the completeness of virological surveillance. Because on-treatment HBV DNA was tested only when hepatitis was clinically suspected, the outcome captured was clinically detected reactivation; the study therefore describes surveillance quality rather than estimating true incidence. With a single event, analyses were kept descriptive. Results Among 139 patients (mean age 60.1 years), 89.2% were anti-HBs-positive and 65.5% received anthracyclines. Baseline HBV DNA was documented in all patients, but serial on-treatment monitoring was not. One clinically overt reactivation occurred (0.7%; 95% CI 0.02%–3.94%) in an anti-HBs-negative woman receiving doxorubicin–cyclophosphamide, presenting as icteric hepatitis at month 4 and responding to entecavir. Since subclinical events would have escaped detection, this figure is best read as a lower bound rather than a true incidence. Conclusions The single reactivation we detected arose in the patient profile that the guidelines flag as moderate-risk—absent anti-HBs together with anthracycline exposure. These observations are consistent with, but do not establish, a risk-adapted approach, given one event and incomplete surveillance. Prospective studies using standardised serial HBV DNA monitoring are needed to estimate true reactivation incidence in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a recognised complication in HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-positive patients receiving cytotoxic cancer therapy, yet real-world data from non-prophylaxis breast cancer practice with incomplete serial HBV DNA surveillance remain scarce.
Design and population
We retrospectively studied HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-positive breast cancer patients treated with systemic therapy between 2018 and 2024 at two centres in Turkey, none of whom received antiviral prophylaxis.
Core methods
We extracted demographic, oncological and virological data, including baseline anti-HBs titres and all available HBV DNA and alanine aminotransferase (ALT) results, and quantified the completeness of virological surveillance.
Main results
Among 139 patients (mean age 60.1 years), 89.2% were anti-HBs-positive and 65.5% received anthracyclines; baseline HBV DNA was documented in all patients, but serial on-treatment monitoring was not. One clinically overt reactivation occurred (0.7%; 95% CI 0.02%–3.94%) in an anti-HBs-negative woman receiving doxorubicin–cyclophosphamide, presenting as icteric hepatitis at month 4 and responding to entecavir.
Interpretation and novelty
The single reactivation detected arose in the patient profile that guidelines flag as moderate-risk—absent anti-HBs together with anthracycline exposure. These observations are consistent with, but do not establish, a risk-adapted approach, given one event and incomplete surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
Prospective studies using standardised serial HBV DNA monitoring are needed to estimate true reactivation incidence in this population, as the figure reported is best read as a lower bound rather than a true incidence due to subclinical events escaping detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is limited by the single event and incomplete surveillance, which prevented estimation of true incidence and may have missed subclinical reactivations, resulting in a descriptive rather than inferential analysis.

STAT3 rs1053004 多态性与伊拉克乙型肝炎患者肝细胞癌的关联。

Association of the STAT3 rs1053004 Polymorphism With Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Iraqi Patients With Hepatitis B.
作者： Hawraa Ahmed Ali, Thuraya Aamer Habeeb, Zahraa F Shaker, Zahraa Q Jasim
摘要中文翻译简介:尽管多年来人们一直在努力预防肝癌,但它仍然是癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。一种已知的危险因素是持续的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染。先前的研究表明 STAT3 信号通路通过炎症机制促进癌发生。在本研究中,我们试图探讨伊拉克 HBV 感染者中 STAT3 rs1053004 多态性与 HCC 发生的相关性。方法:采用病例对照方法,纳入 80 例 HBV 感染 HCC 患者、80 例 CHB 患者和 80 名健康对照者。该研究基于通过 TaqMan SNP 基因分型测定对 STAT3 rs1053004 多态性进行的基因分型。使用多变量逻辑回归分析来评估遗传与疾病风险的关联,并调整潜在的混杂因素。结果:所研究的三组具有显着不同的基因型和等位基因分布(p < 0.001)。HCC 组比其他组具有更多的 T 等位基因。此外,TT 基因型患者患 HCC 的风险更大(调整后比值比 [AOR] = 4.26;95% 置信区间 [CI] = 1.89-9.62;p < 0.001),并且在肝硬化、血清 HBV DNA 水平高和 HBeAg 阴性状态的患者中观察到了这种影响。结论:STAT3 rs1053004 多态性在 HBV 感染者发生 HCC 风险中发挥重要作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管采取了预防措施,肝癌仍然是癌症相关死亡的主要原因,而持续的乙型肝炎病毒感染是已知的危险因素。本研究旨在探讨伊拉克HBV感染者STAT3 rs1053004多态性与HCC发生的相关性。
设计与对象
在伊拉克进行了一项病例对照研究,其中包括 80 名因 HBV 感染而患 HCC 的患者、80 名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者和 80 名健康对照者。
核心方法
该研究使用 TaqMan SNP 基因分型测定对 STAT3 rs1053004 多态性进行基因分型,并使用多元逻辑回归分析来评估与疾病风险的遗传关联,调整潜在的混杂因素。
主要结果
三组的基因型和等位基因分布存在显着差异(p < 0.001),其中 HCC 组具有更多的 T 等位基因。TT 基因型患者患 HCC 的风险较高(AOR = 4.26;95% CI = 1.89-9.62;p < 0.001),尤其是肝硬化、血清 HBV DNA 水平高和 HBeAg 阴性的患者。
解释与创新
作者解释说,STAT3 rs1053004 多态性在 HBV 感染者发生 HCC 风险的过程中发挥着重要作用,凸显了其作为遗传标记的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 HBV 感染人群(尤其是高危亚组)的 HCC 风险基因筛查提供信息,并可以指导个性化 监测 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于伊拉克人群,由于病例对照设计和未完全解决潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Association of the STAT3 rs1053004 Polymorphism With Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Iraqi Patients With Hepatitis B.

Authors: Hawraa Ahmed Ali, Thuraya Aamer Habeeb, Zahraa F Shaker, Zahraa Q Jasim
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: HCC is still one of the main causes of cancer-related fatalities despite years of intensive attempts to prevent it. One known risk factor is a persistent hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Previous studies have indicated a contribution of the STAT3 signaling pathway to carcinogenesis through inflammation mechanisms. In this study, we tried to explore the correlation between STAT3 rs1053004 polymorphism and HCC occurrence in people with HBV infection in Iraq. METHODS: A case-control approach was applied with the inclusion of 80 patients with HCC due to HBV infection, 80 patients with CHB, and 80 healthy controls. The study was based on genotyping of the STAT3 rs1053004 polymorphism by a TaqMan SNP genotyping assay. Multivariate logistic regression analysis was used to assess the genetic association with disease risk, adjusting for potential confounding factors. RESULTS: The three groups under study had significantly different genotype and allele distributions (p < 0.001). The HCC group had more T alleles than the other groups. Additionally, patients with the TT genotype had a greater risk of HCC (adjusted odds ratio [AOR] = 4.26; 95%confidence interval [CI] = 1.89-9.62; p < 0.001), and this impact was observed in patients with cirrhosis, high serum HBV DNA levels, and negative HBeAg status. CONCLUSION: STAT3 rs1053004 polymorphism plays a significant role in the development of HCC risk in people with HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HCC remains a major cause of cancer-related deaths despite prevention efforts, and persistent HBV infection is a known risk factor. This study aimed to explore the correlation between STAT3 rs1053004 polymorphism and HCC occurrence in people with HBV infection in Iraq.
Design and population
A case-control study was conducted with 80 patients with HCC due to HBV infection, 80 patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), and 80 healthy controls in Iraq.
Core methods
The study genotyped the STAT3 rs1053004 polymorphism using a TaqMan SNP genotyping assay and used multivariate logistic regression analysis to assess genetic association with disease risk, adjusting for potential confounding factors.
Main results
The three groups had significantly different genotype and allele distributions (p < 0.001), with the HCC group having more T alleles. Patients with the TT genotype had a greater risk of HCC (AOR = 4.26; 95% CI = 1.89-9.62; p < 0.001), particularly in those with cirrhosis, high serum HBV DNA levels, and negative HBeAg status.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that STAT3 rs1053004 polymorphism plays a significant role in the development of HCC risk in people with HBV infection, highlighting its potential as a genetic marker.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform genetic screening for HCC risk in HBV-infected populations, particularly in high-risk subgroups, and could guide personalized surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to an Iraqi population, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the case-control design and potential confounding factors not fully addressed.

使用炎症作为评估乙型肝炎的框架 疫苗 免疫力 2259206

Using Inflammation as a Framework to Assess Hepatitis B Vaccine Immunity 2259206
作者： Riddhimaa Sinha, Han Chen, Xiaomin Yao, Elizabeth Severa, Tijaana Williams, Ramin Herati
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 尽管 疫苗 可以预防近四十年,但 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题。尽管 疫苗接种 显着降低了疾病负担,但仍有一部分人未能形成足够的保护,并被归类为无反应者。方法 一项纵向研究评估了既往有或没有 疫苗接种 的个体的乙型肝炎 疫苗 反应。参与者接受了 3 剂明矾佐剂或 2 剂 CpG 佐剂 疫苗,其中一部分接受了加强剂。ELISA 分析了第 1、8 和 30 天收集的血液样本,以量化抗 HBs 滴度,分为高 (≥50 mIU/mL)、中 (10-50 mIU/mL) 或低 (≤10 mIU/mL)。使用改良的 ELISA 和离液剂来测量抗体亲合力,以评估结合强度。结果年龄显着影响 疫苗 反应。在加强组中,50 岁以下的参与者具有较高的基线滴度,而在从头组中,较年轻的个体表现出更强的早期反应,尽管不同年龄段的最终滴度相似。在高血压参与者中,老年人表现出较慢但稳定的抗体增加,在第 4 周超过年轻参与者。两个 疫苗 组的亲合力保持稳定,表明先前的 疫苗 增加了抗体数量,但没有增加结合强度,并在早期达到稳定水平。结论 先前接种过疫苗的个体的免疫记忆是可变的。尽管加强剂产生了更快的抗体上升,但无反应者比例较高,这表明之前的 疫苗接种 并不总是能确保持久的保护。高血压也影响了 疫苗 反应:虽然高血压个体实现了早期血清转换,但其抗体水平稳定在较低水平,表明免疫维持受损可能与血管炎症或内皮功能障碍有关。总体而言,这些发现强调了针对高血压或高龄个体的个性化 疫苗接种 策略的需要。资金来源 本摘要中报告的研究得到了美国国立卫生研究院国家过敏和传染病研究所的支持,奖项编号为 AI158617。此外,还得到 Hevolution/AFAR 新研究者奖主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC) 的支持
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 疫苗 可以预防近四十年,但 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题。尽管 疫苗接种 显着降低了疾病负担,但仍有一部分人未能形成足够的保护,并被归类为无反应者。
设计与对象
一项纵向研究评估了先前有或没有 疫苗接种 的个体的乙型肝炎 疫苗 反应。参与者接受了 3 剂明矾佐剂或 2 剂 CpG 佐剂 疫苗,其中一部分接受了加强剂。
核心方法
ELISA 分析了第 1、8 和 30 天收集的血液样本,以量化抗 HBs 滴度,分为高 (≥50 mIU/mL)、中 (10-50 mIU/mL) 或低 (≤10 mIU/mL)。使用改良的 ELISA 和离液剂来测量抗体亲合力,以评估结合强度。
主要结果
年龄显着影响 疫苗 反应。在加强组中,50 岁以下的参与者具有较高的基线滴度,而在从头组中,较年轻的个体表现出更强的早期反应,尽管不同年龄段的最终滴度相似。在高血压参与者中,老年人表现出较慢但稳定的抗体增加,在第 4 周超过年轻参与者。两个 疫苗 组的亲合力保持稳定,表明先前的 疫苗 增加了抗体数量,但没有增加结合强度,并在早期达到稳定水平。
解释与创新
先前接种过疫苗的个体的免疫记忆是可变的。尽管加强剂产生了更快的抗体上升,但无反应者比例较高,这表明之前的 疫苗接种 并不总是能确保持久的保护。
科研与公卫意义
高血压也塑造了 疫苗 反应:虽然高血压个体实现了早期血清转化,但他们的抗体水平稳定在较低水平,表明免疫维持受损可能与血管炎症或内皮功能障碍有关。总体而言,这些发现强调了针对高血压或高龄患者制定个性化 疫苗接种 策略的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的局限性或证据的强度。该研究得到了美国国立卫生研究院国家过敏和传染病研究所的支持,奖项编号为 AI158617,并得到了 Hevolution/AFAR 新研究者奖主题类别 疫苗 和免疫治疗 (VAC) 的支持。

Using Inflammation as a Framework to Assess Hepatitis B Vaccine Immunity 2259206

Authors: Riddhimaa Sinha, Han Chen, Xiaomin Yao, Elizabeth Severa, Tijaana Williams, Ramin Herati
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Infection with hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global health concern despite being vaccine-preventable for nearly four decades. Although vaccination has significantly reduced disease burden, a subset of individuals fails to develop adequate protection and are classified as non-responders. Methods A longitudinal study evaluated hepatitis B vaccine responses in individuals with or without prior vaccination. Participants received either a 3-dose alum-adjuvanted or 2-dose CpG-adjuvanted vaccine, with a subset receiving a booster. ELISA analyzed blood samples collected on days 1, 8, and 30 to quantify anti-HBs titers, classified as high (≥50 mIU/mL), moderate (10—50 mIU/mL), or low (≤10 mIU/mL). Antibody avidity was measured using a modified ELISA with a chaotropic agent to assess binding strength. Results Age significantly affected vaccine responses. In the booster group, participants under 50 had higher baseline titers, while in the de novo group, younger individuals showed stronger early responses though final titers were similar across ages. Among hypertensive participants, older adults showed slower but steady antibody increases, surpassing younger participants by week 4. Avidity remained stable across both vaccine groups, indicating that prior vaccination increased antibody quantity but not binding strength, which plateaued early. Conclusion Immune recall among previously vaccinated individuals was variable. Although boosters produced a faster antibody rise, a higher proportion of non-responders suggests that prior vaccination does not always ensure lasting protection. Hypertension also shaped vaccine response: while hypertensive individuals achieved early seroconversion, their antibody levels plateaued at lower magnitudes, suggesting impaired immune sustainment possibly linked to vascular inflammation or endothelial dysfunction.Overall, these findings highlight the need for personalized vaccination strategies for individuals with hypertension or advanced age. Funding Source Research reported in this abstract was supported by the National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases of the National Institutes of Health under Award Number AI158617. Additionally, supported by Hevolution/AFAR New Investigator Award Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infection with hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global health concern despite being vaccine-preventable for nearly four decades. Although vaccination has significantly reduced disease burden, a subset of individuals fails to develop adequate protection and are classified as non-responders.
Design and population
A longitudinal study evaluated hepatitis B vaccine responses in individuals with or without prior vaccination. Participants received either a 3-dose alum-adjuvanted or 2-dose CpG-adjuvanted vaccine, with a subset receiving a booster.
Core methods

Main results
Age significantly affected vaccine responses. In the booster group, participants under 50 had higher baseline titers, while in the de novo group, younger individuals showed stronger early responses though final titers were similar across ages. Among hypertensive participants, older adults showed slower but steady antibody increases, surpassing younger participants by week 4. Avidity remained stable across both vaccine groups, indicating that prior vaccination increased antibody quantity but not binding strength, which plateaued early.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Hypertension also shaped vaccine response: while hypertensive individuals achieved early seroconversion, their antibody levels plateaued at lower magnitudes, suggesting impaired immune sustainment possibly linked to vascular inflammation or endothelial dysfunction. Overall, these findings highlight the need for personalized vaccination strategies for individuals with hypertension or advanced age.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or the strength of the evidence. HIV感染者和未感染者在自发控制后乙型肝炎中和抗体轨迹存在差异

Divergent hepatitis B neutralizing antibody trajectories after spontaneous control in people with and without HIV
作者： Chloe L. Thio, Che‐Min Lo, Eric Seaberg, Mark Anderson, Maraake Taddese, Jessica Mimms, Ernesto T. A. Marques, Mallory Witt, Steven M. Wolinsky, Gavin Cloherty
摘要中文翻译摘要 HIV 感染者 (PWH) 对急性 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的自发控制 (SC) 降低,并且 HBV 再激活的风险增加,但针对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (抗 HBs) 的保护性抗体在 SC 后是否仍保持功能尚不清楚。我们分析了 MACS/WIHS 联合 队列研究 研究中 136 名男性的纵向抗体反应,其中有 HBV 事件,随后进行 SC(99 名无 HIV 患者 [PWoH],37 名 PWH)在第一次就诊时与 SC 一致,并在大约 6 个月和 30 个月后进行。SC 后 2.5 年内,PWoH 中的抗 HBs 滴度有所增加,但 PWH 中的抗 HBs 滴度没有增加。同样,在 PWoH 中 SC 后 HBV 中和度较高并保持稳定,而在 PWH 中呈下降趋势。相比之下,两组中针对核心抗原的抗体均下降相似。出乎意料的是,乙型肝炎表面抗原-抗 HBs 免疫复合物 (HBsAg IC) 存在于大多数样本中,并且随着时间的推移保持稳定。这些发现支持 SC 后持续产生 HBsAg,这导致 PWoH 中受到 HIV 损害的功能性抗 HBs 控制增强,这可能解释了 HIV 对恢复和再激活风险的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查感染和未感染艾滋病毒的人在急性 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染自发控制 (SC) 后,针对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (抗-HBs) 的保护性抗体是否仍保持功能。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 MACS/WIHS 联合 队列研究 研究中 136 名男性的纵向抗体反应,其中包括 99 名未感染 HIV 的人 (PWoH) 和 37 名经历过 HBV 感染并随后自发控制的 HIV 感染者 (PWH)。
核心方法
在第一次就诊时评估纵向抗体反应,与自发对照一致,并在大约 6 个月和 30 个月后评估,重点关注抗 HBs 滴度、HBV 中和、核心抗原抗体和 HBsAg-抗 HBs 免疫复合物。
主要结果
在 PWoH 中 SC 后 2.5 年内,抗 HBs 滴度增加,但在 PWH 中则没有增加。PWoH 中的 HBV 中和水平较高且稳定,而 PWH 中则呈下降趋势。两组中针对核心抗原的抗体都有类似的下降。大多数样本中都存在 HBsAg-抗 HBs 免疫复合物,并且随着时间的推移保持稳定。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,SC 后持续产生的 HBsAg 导致 PWoH 中功能性抗 HBs 控制的增加,而这种控制受到 HIV 的损害,这可能解释了 HIV 对恢复和再激活风险的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于了解 HIV 如何影响 HBV 恢复和再激活风险,并对 监测 和 HIV 阳性 HBV 患者的管理策略产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于特定人群和时间范围,证据仅基于抽象数据,可能缺乏详细的统计或方法学验证。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

Divergent hepatitis B neutralizing antibody trajectories after spontaneous control in people with and without HIV

Authors: Chloe L. Thio, Che‐Min Lo, Eric Seaberg, Mark Anderson, Maraake Taddese, Jessica Mimms, Ernesto T. A. Marques, Mallory Witt, Steven M. Wolinsky, Gavin Cloherty
Original abstract (English)
Abstract People with HIV (PWH) have reduced spontaneous control (SC) of acute hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and increased risk of HBV reactivation, but whether protective antibodies against hepatitis B surface antigen (anti-HBs) remain functional after SC is unclear. We analyzed longitudinal antibody responses of 136 men in the MACS/WIHS Combined Cohort Study with incident HBV followed by SC (99 people without HIV [PWoH], 37 PWH) at the first visit consistent with SC and approximately 6 and 30 months later. Anti HBs titers increased over 2.5 years after SC in PWoH but not PWH. Similarly, HBV neutralization was higher after SC in PWoH and remained stable, while trending downward in PWH. In contrast, antibodies against core antigen declined similarly in both groups. Unexpectedly, hepatitis B surface antigen–anti-HBs immune complexes (HBsAg ICs) were present in the majority of samples and remained stable over time. These findings support persistent HBsAg production after SC, which leads to increasing functional anti-HBs control in PWoH that is impaired by HIV potentially explaining effects of HIV on recovery and reactivation risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether protective antibodies against hepatitis B surface antigen (anti-HBs) remain functional after spontaneous control (SC) of acute hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in people with and without HIV.
Design and population
The study analyzed longitudinal antibody responses from 136 men in the MACS/WIHS Combined Cohort Study, including 99 people without HIV (PWoH) and 37 people with HIV (PWH) who experienced incident HBV followed by spontaneous control.
Core methods
Longitudinal antibody responses were assessed at the first visit consistent with spontaneous control and approximately 6 and 30 months later, focusing on anti-HBs titers, HBV neutralization, core antigen antibodies, and HBsAg-anti-HBs immune complexes.
Main results
Anti-HBs titers increased over 2.5 years after SC in PWoH but not in PWH. HBV neutralization was higher and stable in PWoH, while it trended downward in PWH. Antibodies against core antigen declined similarly in both groups. HBsAg-anti-HBs immune complexes were present in the majority of samples and remained stable over time.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that persistent HBsAg production after SC leads to increasing functional anti-HBs control in PWoH, which is impaired by HIV, potentially explaining effects of HIV on recovery and reactivation risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform understanding of how HIV affects HBV recovery and reactivation risk, with implications for surveillance and management strategies in HIV-positive individuals with HBV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a specific population and timeframe, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may lack detailed statistical or methodological validation. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

多级医院干预对接受免疫治疗的神经炎症患者的肝炎筛查和风险管理的影响。

Impact of a multilevel hospital intervention on hepatitis screening and risk management in neuroinflammatory patients receiving immune therapy.
作者： Yi-Ting Fang, Che-Chun Yeh, Meng-Ju Wu, Po-Yu Lin, Chun-Min Wang, Hsien-Jy Ma, Sheng-Hsiang Lin, Yuan-Ting Sun, Ting-Tsung Chang
摘要中文翻译背景:接受免疫治疗的神经炎症性疾病患者面临 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 再激活的风险,特别是在流行地区。然而,在神经病学实践中,在免疫治疗之前遵守推荐的肝炎筛查仍然不一致。这项研究评估了基于医院的干预措施是否可以改善该人群的肝炎筛查和风险管理。方法:我们在台湾一家三级医疗中心和一家地区医院的神经内科进行了一项回顾性-前瞻性 队列研究 研究。干预措施包括临床医生教育、基于电子病历的筛查警报以及对筛查依从性的定期反馈。比较干预前(2018年12月至2021年5月)和干预后(2021年6月至2023年11月)队列研究的筛查率、筛查及时性和临床管理行动。结果:共有 344 名接受神经炎症性疾病免疫治疗的患者纳入研究。干预后,免疫治疗前的 HBsAg(37.1% 至 63.3%)、抗 HBc(10.1% 至 31.8%)和抗 HCV(34.3% 至 59.8%)筛查率显着提高(均 p < 0.001)。包括治疗中检测在内的总体筛查率也大幅增加。筛查时间分析表明,干预后治疗中筛查的延迟时间较短。HBV 鉴定后的临床反应(包括 HBV DNA 检测、肝病转诊和 抗病毒药物 预防)在干预后时期明显更加频繁。改进的筛查还揭示了以前未被识别的乙肝病毒携带和再激活事件。结论:基于医院的结构化干预结合了临床医生教育、电子提醒和反馈,显着改善了接受免疫治疗的神经炎症患者的肝炎筛查和下游临床管理。这些发现支持将系统性肝炎风险评估纳入 HBV/HCV 流行地区的神经护理中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
接受免疫治疗的神经炎症性疾病患者面临着 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活的风险,特别是在流行地区,但在神经病学实践中,治疗前遵守推荐的 HBV 筛查的情况仍然不一致。
设计与对象
一项回顾性-前瞻性队列研究研究在台湾一家三级医疗中心和一家地方医院的神经内科进行,比较了接受神经炎症性疾病免疫治疗的患者干预前(2018年12月至2021年5月)和干预后(2021年6月至2023年11月)的队列研究。
核心方法
干预措施包括临床医生教育、基于电子病历的筛查警报以及筛查依从性的定期反馈,并在 队列研究 之间评估筛查的采用率、及时性和临床管理行动。
主要结果
在 344 名患者中,干预后 HBsAg(37.1% 至 63.3%)、抗 HBc(10.1% 至 31.8%)和抗 HCV(34.3% 至 59.8%)(均 p < 0.001)干预后免疫治疗前筛查率显着提高,总体筛查率和治疗中检测也大幅增加。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,整合教育、电子提醒和反馈的结构化医院干预措施显着改善了这一高危人群的肝炎筛查和下游临床管理。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持将系统性肝炎风险评估纳入乙型肝炎病毒流行地区的神经护理中,以减轻免疫治疗期间的再激活风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性前瞻性设计、单一国家背景和缺乏随机性而受到限制,限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要对证据有适度的信心。

Impact of a multilevel hospital intervention on hepatitis screening and risk management in neuroinflammatory patients receiving immune therapy.

Authors: Yi-Ting Fang, Che-Chun Yeh, Meng-Ju Wu, Po-Yu Lin, Chun-Min Wang, Hsien-Jy Ma, Sheng-Hsiang Lin, Yuan-Ting Sun, Ting-Tsung Chang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Patients with neuroinflammatory disorders receiving immune therapy are at risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) reactivation, particularly in endemic regions. However, adherence to recommended hepatitis screening prior to immune therapy remains inconsistent in neurological practice. This study evaluated whether a hospital-based intervention could improve hepatitis screening and risk management in this population. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective-prospective cohort study in the neurology departments of a tertiary medical center and a regional hospital in Taiwan. The intervention included clinician education, electronic medical record-based screening alerts, and periodic feedback on screening adherence. Screening uptake, screening timeliness, and clinical management actions were compared between pre-intervention (December 2018-May 2021) and post-intervention (June 2021-November 2023) cohorts. RESULTS: A total of 344 patients receiving immune therapy for neuroinflammatory disorders were included. Pre-immune therapy screening rates improved significantly after the intervention for HBsAg (37.1% to 63.3%), anti-HBc (10.1% to 31.8%), and anti-HCV (34.3% to 59.8%) (all p < 0.001). Overall screening rates, including in-therapy testing, also increased substantially. Time-to-screening analysis demonstrated shorter delays for in-therapy screening after the intervention. Clinical responses following HBV identification-including HBV DNA testing, hepatology referral, and antiviral prophylaxis-were significantly more frequent in the post-intervention period. Improved screening also revealed previously unrecognized HBV carriage and reactivation events. CONCLUSIONS: A structured hospital-based intervention integrating clinician education, electronic reminders, and feedback significantly improved hepatitis screening and downstream clinical management in neuroinflammatory patients receiving immune therapy. These findings support incorporating systematic hepatitis risk assessment into neurological care in HBV/HCV-endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Patients with neuroinflammatory disorders receiving immune therapy face risks of hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation, particularly in endemic regions, yet adherence to recommended HBV screening before therapy remains inconsistent in neurological practice.
Design and population
A retrospective-prospective cohort study was conducted in the neurology departments of a tertiary medical center and a regional hospital in Taiwan, comparing pre-intervention (December 2018–May 2021) and post-intervention (June 2021–November 2023) cohorts of patients receiving immune therapy for neuroinflammatory disorders.
Core methods
The intervention consisted of clinician education, electronic medical record-based screening alerts, and periodic feedback on screening adherence, with screening uptake, timeliness, and clinical management actions assessed between cohorts.
Main results
Among 344 patients, pre-immune therapy screening rates improved significantly after the intervention for HBsAg (37.1% to 63.3%), anti-HBc (10.1% to 31.8%), and anti-HCV (34.3% to 59.8%) (all p < 0.001), with overall screening rates and in-therapy testing also increasing substantially.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that a structured hospital-based intervention integrating education, electronic reminders, and feedback significantly improved hepatitis screening and downstream clinical management in this high-risk population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support incorporating systematic hepatitis risk assessment into neurological care in HBV-endemic regions to mitigate reactivation risks during immune therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective-prospective design, single-country setting, and lack of randomization, which constrain causal inference and generalizability, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者 HBsAg 消失的预测模型的构建和验证

Construction and validation of a predictive model for HBsAg loss in chronic hepatitis B patients treated with Peg-IFNα-2b
作者： Xinrui Ren, Xinyi Sheng, Zhengbinkelvin Wong, Yu Cui, Qi Zhang, Jiayi Song, Wanchun Zhu, Yueqiu Gao, Jianjun Hu, Lingying Huang
摘要中文翻译背景 乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 丧失是 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的理想 治疗 终点。聚乙二醇化干扰素α-2b(Peg-IFNα-2b)是目前实现HBsAg消失的首选药物,但其疗效在不同患者之间存在显着差异。方法 这项多中心、回顾性真实世界研究纳入了接受 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗≥48 周的慢性乙型肝炎患者。根据第 48 周 HBsAg 状态将患者分为治愈组和未治愈组,并随机分配至训练和验证 队列研究 (7:3)。逻辑回归确定了 HBsAg 消失的独立预测因素并构建了预测模型。使用受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析、校准曲线、Hosmer-Lemeshow检验和决策分析曲线(DCA)分析来评估模型的判别能力、校准和临床适用性。结果 在 859 名患者中,133 名患者 (15.5%) 在第 48 周时实现 HBsAg 消失。多变量分析确定中药 (TCM) 联合治疗 (OR = 3.83)、较低基线 HBsAg (OR = 0.32)、较低基线天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST) (OR = 0.97) 和恩替卡韦 (ETV) 方案 (OR = 0.33) 作为独立预测因素。该模型表现出强大的辨别和校准性能,训练 队列研究 中的 AUC 为 0.884,验证 队列研究 中的 AUC 为 0.866。Logit(P)=-0.87-1.12*HBsAg(log10)+1.34*group-0.03*AST-1.1×ETV。注:组(1=不与中医联合,2=与中医联合),ETV(0=非ETV 抗病毒药物方案,1=ETV 抗病毒药物方案)。结论 中医治疗、低基线 HBsAg 水平、较低基线 AST 水平和非 ETV 抗病毒药物 方案的组合是接受 48 周 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗 治疗的 CHB 患者在 48 周时 HBsAg 消失的显着预测因素。我们开发的预测模型表现出较高的准确性和潜在的临床实用性。要点本研究的重点是基于包含 859 名 CHB 患者的大型两中心 队列研究 建立和验证 HBsAg 丢失的临床预测模型。该模型是使用严格的统计方法构建的,并通过内部和外部验证进一步评估。结果显示出良好的区分度、校准和临床净效益,表明该模型可以作为实用工具来帮助临床医生估计 HBsAg 丢失的概率并促进个体化 抗病毒药物 治疗 策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 丧失是 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的理想 治疗 终点。聚乙二醇化干扰素α-2b(Peg-IFNα-2b)是目前实现HBsAg消失的首选药物,但其疗效在不同患者之间存在显着差异。
设计与对象
这项多中心、回顾性现实世界研究纳入了接受 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗≥48 周的慢性乙型肝炎患者。根据第 48 周 HBsAg 状态将患者分为治愈组和未治愈组,并随机分配至训练和验证 队列研究 (7:3)。
核心方法
逻辑回归确定了 HBsAg 消失的独立预测因素并构建了预测模型。使用受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析、校准曲线、Hosmer-Lemeshow 检验和决策分析曲线(DCA)分析来评估模型的判别能力、校准和临床适用性。
主要结果
在 859 名患者中,133 名患者 (15.5%) 在第 48 周实现 HBsAg 消失。多变量分析确定中药 (TCM) 联合治疗 (OR = 3.83)、较低基线 HBsAg (OR = 0.32)、较低基线天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST) (OR = 0.97) 和恩替卡韦 (ETV) 方案 (OR = 0.33) 作为独立预测因素。该模型表现出强大的辨别和校准性能,训练 队列研究 中的 AUC 为 0.884,验证 队列研究 中的 AUC 为 0.866。
解释与创新
中医治疗、低基线 HBsAg 水平、较低基线 AST 水平和非 ETV 抗病毒药物 方案的组合是接受 48 周 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗 治疗的 CHB 患者在第 48 周时 HBsAg 消失的显着预测因子。开发的预测模型表现出较高的准确性和潜在的临床实用性。
科研与公卫意义
该模型可以作为实用工具帮助临床医生估计 HBsAg 丢失的概率并促进个体化 抗病毒药物 治疗 策略。这可以通过确定最有可能从 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗中受益的患者来改善 治疗 的结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性现实世界分析,可能会引入潜在的偏差。然而,大样本量(859 名患者)和严格的统计方法(包括内部和外部验证)为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

Construction and validation of a predictive model for HBsAg loss in chronic hepatitis B patients treated with Peg-IFNα-2b

Authors: Xinrui Ren, Xinyi Sheng, Zhengbinkelvin Wong, Yu Cui, Qi Zhang, Jiayi Song, Wanchun Zhu, Yueqiu Gao, Jianjun Hu, Lingying Huang
Original abstract (English)
Background Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss is the ideal treatment endpoint for chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Pegylated interferon alfa-2b (Peg-IFNα-2b) is currently the preferred drug for achieving HBsAg loss, but its efficacy varies significantly among different patients. Methods This multicenter, retrospective real-world study enrolled CHB patients treated with Peg-IFNα-2b for ≥48 weeks. Patients were divided into cured and uncured groups based on week-48 HBsAg status and randomly assigned to training and validation cohorts (7:3). Logistic regression identified independent predictors of HBsAg loss and constructed a predictive model. The model’s discriminative ability, calibration, and clinical applicability were evaluated using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis, calibration curves, Hosmer–Lemeshow test, and decision analysis curve (DCA) analysis. Results Among 859 patients, 133 (15.5%) achieved HBsAg loss at week 48. Multivariate analysis identified combination therapy with traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) (OR = 3.83), lower baseline HBsAg (OR = 0.32), lower baseline aspartate aminotransferase (AST) (OR = 0.97), and entecavir (ETV) regimen (OR = 0.33) as independent predictors. The model demonstrated strong discrimination and calibration performance, with AUCs of 0.884 in the training cohort and 0.866 in the validation cohort. Logit(P)=-0.87-1.12*HBsAg(log10)+1.34*group-0.03*AST-1.1×ETV. Note: group (1=no combination with TCM, 2=combination with TCM), ETV (0=non-ETV antiviral regimen, 1=ETV antiviral regimen). Conclusion The combination of TCM therapy, low baseline HBsAg levels, lower baseline AST levels, and non-ETV antiviral regimens were significant predictors of HBsAg loss at week 48 in CHB patients receiving 48-week Peg-IFNα-2b treatment. The predictive model we developed demonstrated high accuracy and potential clinical utility. Key points This study focused on the establishment and validation of a clinical prediction model for HBsAg loss based on a large two-center cohort including 859 patients with CHB. The model was constructed using rigorous statistical methods and further evaluated through both internal and external validation. The results demonstrated good discrimination, calibration, and clinical net benefit, suggesting that the model may serve as a practical tool to help clinicians estimate the probability of HBsAg loss and facilitate individualized antiviral treatment strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss is the ideal treatment endpoint for chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Pegylated interferon alfa-2b (Peg-IFNα-2b) is currently the preferred drug for achieving HBsAg loss, but its efficacy varies significantly among different patients.
Design and population
This multicenter, retrospective real-world study enrolled CHB patients treated with Peg-IFNα-2b for ≥48 weeks. Patients were divided into cured and uncured groups based on week-48 HBsAg status and randomly assigned to training and validation cohorts (7:3).
Core methods
Logistic regression identified independent predictors of HBsAg loss and constructed a predictive model. The model's discriminative ability, calibration, and clinical applicability were evaluated using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis, calibration curves, Hosmer–Lemeshow test, and decision analysis curve (DCA) analysis.
Main results
Among 859 patients, 133 (15.5%) achieved HBsAg loss at week 48. Multivariate analysis identified combination therapy with traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) (OR = 3.83), lower baseline HBsAg (OR = 0.32), lower baseline aspartate aminotransferase (AST) (OR = 0.97), and entecavir (ETV) regimen (OR = 0.33) as independent predictors. The model demonstrated strong discrimination and calibration performance, with AUCs of 0.884 in the training cohort and 0.866 in the validation cohort.
Interpretation and novelty
The combination of TCM therapy, low baseline HBsAg levels, lower baseline AST levels, and non-ETV antiviral regimens were significant predictors of HBsAg loss at week 48 in CHB patients receiving 48-week Peg-IFNα-2b treatment. The predictive model developed demonstrated high accuracy and potential clinical utility.
Scientific and public-health significance
The model may serve as a practical tool to help clinicians estimate the probability of HBsAg loss and facilitate individualized antiviral treatment strategies. This could improve treatment outcomes by identifying patients most likely to benefit from Peg-IFNα-2b therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a retrospective real-world analysis, which may introduce potential biases. However, the large sample size (859 patients) and rigorous statistical methods, including both internal and external validation, provide moderate confidence in the findings.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

间充质干细胞的精确免疫调节可减轻 乙型肝炎病毒 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞介导的急性肝损伤,且不影响病毒清除

Precise immunomodulation by mesenchymal stem cells mitigates hepatitis B virus-specific CD8+ T cell-mediated acute liver injury without compromising viral clearance
作者： Xingxing Li, Zhiwei Li, Qibin Liao, Yanhong Huang, Shumin Liao, Liang Li
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 相关慢性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 与高 死亡率 发生率相关。尽管间充质干细胞(MSC)治疗可显着改善 HBV-ACLF 患者的肝功能和生存率,但 MSC 的免疫调节作用是否会抑制 抗病毒药物 CD8+ T 细胞功能并触发 HBV 再激活仍不清楚。在这里,我们开发了一种新型体外 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞介导的急性肝损伤类器官模型,该模型与经血来源的 MSC(MenSC)或脐带来源的 MSC(UC-MSC)共培养。我们发现 MenSC 和 UC-MSC 都通过精确的免疫调节(主要是通过旁分泌信号传导)来协调肝脏修复和 抗病毒药物 免疫。两种类型的 MSC 均显着下调促炎基因(IFN-γ、TNF-α、IL-8 和 CXCL10)和凋亡相关基因(FasL 和 CASP7),同时通过上调 IL-6 和 HGF 促进肝再生,且不影响 CD8+ T 细胞介导的 HBV 清除。这项研究为 MSC 治疗 HBV-ACLF 的安全性提供了重要见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 相关的慢加急性肝衰竭 (HBV-ACLF) 与高 死亡率 发生率相关,尽管间充质干细胞 (MSC) 治疗可改善肝功能和生存率,但尚不清楚 MSC 是否会抑制 抗病毒药物 CD8+ T 细胞功能并触发 HBV 再激活。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种体外 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞介导的急性肝损伤类器官模型,与经血来源的 MSC (MenSC) 或脐带来源的 MSC (UC-MSC) 共培养。
核心方法
该研究使用体外 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞介导的急性肝损伤类器官模型,与 MenSC 或 UC-MSC 共培养,以研究它们对肝脏修复和 抗病毒药物 免疫的影响。
主要结果
MenSC 和 UC-MSC 均通过精确的免疫调节(主要通过旁分泌信号传导)协调肝脏修复和 抗病毒药物 免疫,而不影响 CD8+ T 细胞介导的 HBV 清除。
解释与创新
两种 MSC 类型均显着下调促炎和凋亡相关基因,同时通过上调 IL-6 和 HGF 促进肝再生,且不影响 CD8+ T 细胞介导的 HBV 清除。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究为 MSC 治疗 HBV-ACLF 的安全性提供了重要见解,表明 MSC 治疗可以减轻肝损伤而不影响病毒清除。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外模型,可能无法完全复制复杂的体内环境;然而,这些发现为 HBV-ACLF 中 MSC 治疗的安全性提供了中等证据。

Precise immunomodulation by mesenchymal stem cells mitigates hepatitis B virus-specific CD8+ T cell-mediated acute liver injury without compromising viral clearance

Authors: Xingxing Li, Zhiwei Li, Qibin Liao, Yanhong Huang, Shumin Liao, Liang Li
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) is associated with a high mortality rate. Although mesenchymal stem cell (MSC) therapy markedly improves liver function and survival in patients with HBV-ACLF, it remains unclear whether the immunomodulatory effects of MSCs suppress antiviral CD8+ T cell function and trigger HBV reactivation. Here, we developed a novel in vitro HBV-specific CD8+ T cell–mediated acute liver injury organoid model, which was co-cultured with either menstrual blood-derived MSCs (MenSCs) or umbilical cord-derived MSCs (UC-MSCs). We found that both MenSCs and UC-MSCs coordinate liver repair and antiviral immunity through precise immunomodulation, primarily via paracrine signaling. Both MSC types significantly downregulated pro-inflammatory ( IFN-γ , TNF-α , IL-8 , and CXCL10 ) and apoptosis-related genes ( FasL and CASP7 ), while promoting hepatic regeneration through IL-6 and HGF upregulation, without compromising CD8+ T cell-mediated HBV clearance. This study provides critical insights into the safety of MSC therapy for HBV-ACLF.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus-related acute-on-chronic liver failure (HBV-ACLF) is associated with a high mortality rate, and although mesenchymal stem cell (MSC) therapy improves liver function and survival, it is unclear whether MSCs suppress antiviral CD8+ T cell function and trigger HBV reactivation.
Design and population
The study developed an in vitro HBV-specific CD8+ T cell–mediated acute liver injury organoid model co-cultured with either menstrual blood-derived MSCs (MenSCs) or umbilical cord-derived MSCs (UC-MSCs).
Core methods
The study used an in vitro HBV-specific CD8+ T cell–mediated acute liver injury organoid model co-cultured with either MenSCs or UC-MSCs to investigate their effects on liver repair and antiviral immunity.
Main results
Both MenSCs and UC-MSCs coordinated liver repair and antiviral immunity through precise immunomodulation, primarily via paracrine signaling, without compromising CD8+ T cell-mediated HBV clearance.
Interpretation and novelty
Both MSC types significantly downregulated pro-inflammatory and apoptosis-related genes while promoting hepatic regeneration through IL-6 and HGF upregulation, without compromising CD8+ T cell-mediated HBV clearance.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides critical insights into the safety of MSC therapy for HBV-ACLF, suggesting that MSC therapy can mitigate liver injury without compromising viral clearance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an in vitro model, which may not fully replicate the complex in vivo environment; however, the findings provide moderate evidence for the safety of MSC therapy in HBV-ACLF.

患病率、发病率 与土耳其红新月会献血者的 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 残留风险:从实施核酸检测开始的一项为期 9 年的调查(2015-2023 年)

Prevalence, incidence and residual risk of HBV, HCV and HIV in Turkish Red Crescent blood donors: A 9‐year survey initiated with the implementation of nucleic acid testing (2015–2023)
作者： Mehmet Bakır Saygan, Soner Yilmaz, Can Murat Beker, Özgür Rüştü Güner, Fatma Meriç Yilmaz, Güner ÖR
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:核酸检测(NAT)通过缩短输血传播感染的诊断窗口期(WP)来提高血液安全性。在基于 NAT 的国家筛查计划的背景下,本研究评估了 2015 年至 2023 年间土耳其红新月会献血者中 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的时间趋势。材料和方法:对 血清学 和 NAT 筛查的 22,494,116 份献血进行了回顾性分析。使用标准 发病率/WP 模型估计 发病率、发病率 和残余风险 (RR)。使用线性回归评估时间趋势,并累积评估 NAT 产量。结果:总体确认的 HBV 阳性率(每 100,000 名捐献者)为 288.5,HCV 为 16.2,HIV 为 14.8。HBV 和 HCV 呈显着下降趋势(p < 0.01),而 HIV 则显​​着上升(p < 0.01)。HBV、HCV 和 HIV 的累计 NAT 产量分别为每百万份献血 189.35、2.94 和 2.15。2015 年至 2023 年间,乙肝病毒(634.79 至 238.34)和丙肝病毒(1.69 至 0.99)的每百万次捐赠的估计总体 RR 有所下降,但艾滋病病毒的估计总体 RR 有所上升(4.08 至 11.40)。值得注意的是,HIV RR 的上升(与 HCV 的下降形成鲜明对比)是由重复捐赠者 发病率 的增加以及用于风险评估的 WP 模型的差异驱动的。结论:虽然基于 NAT 的筛查与 HBV 和 HCV 的 RR 较低且不断下降相关,但 HIV 的上升轨迹令人担忧。这强调需要加强行为筛查和捐助者选择策略以及分子检测,以应对不断上升的艾滋病毒风险。

问题与背景
背景和目的:核酸检测(NAT)通过缩短输血传播感染的诊断窗口期(WP)来提高血液安全性。

在基于 NAT 的国家筛查计划的背景下,本研究评估了 2015 年至 2023 年间土耳其红新月会献血者中 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的时间趋势。
核心方法

总体确认的 HBV 阳性率(每 100,000 名捐献者)为 288.5,HCV 为 16.2,HIV 为 14.8。HBV 和 HCV 呈显着下降趋势(p < 0.01),而 HIV 则显​​着上升(p < 0.01)。HBV、HCV 和 HIV 的累计 NAT 产量分别为每百万份献血 189.35、2.94 和 2.15。2015 年至 2023 年间,乙肝病毒(634.79 至 238.34)和丙肝病毒(1.69 至 0.99)的每百万次捐赠的估计总体 RR 有所下降,但艾滋病病毒的估计总体 RR 有所上升(4.08 至 11.40)。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果证明 NAT 在降低 HBV 和 HCV 残留风险方面的有效性,为血液安全策略提供参考,同时强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决献血者中日益增加的 HIV 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对残余风险估计数学模型的依赖而受到限制。由于样本量较大,证据强度中等,但由于报告数据仅摘要性质而受到削弱。

Prevalence, incidence and residual risk of HBV, HCV and HIV in Turkish Red Crescent blood donors: A 9‐year survey initiated with the implementation of nucleic acid testing (2015–2023)

Authors: Mehmet Bakır Saygan, Soner Yilmaz, Can Murat Beker, Özgür Rüştü Güner, Fatma Meriç Yilmaz, Güner ÖR
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Nucleic acid testing (NAT) has improved blood safety by shortening the diagnostic window period (WP) for transfusion-transmissible infections. Within the context of a national NAT-based screening programme, this study evaluated temporal trends for hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV) and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) among Turkish Red Crescent blood donors between 2015 and 2023. MATERIALS AND METHODS: A retrospective analysis was conducted on 22,494,116 blood donations screened by serology and NAT. Prevalence, incidence and residual risk (RR) were estimated using standard incidence/WP models. Temporal trends were evaluated using linear regression, and NAT yield was assessed cumulatively. RESULTS: The overall confirmed positivity (per 100,000 donors) was 288.5 for HBV, 16.2 for HCV and 14.8 for HIV. HBV and HCV showed significant downward trends (p < 0.01), while HIV rose significantly (p < 0.01). Cumulative NAT yields were 189.35, 2.94 and 2.15 per million donations for HBV, HCV and HIV, respectively. Between 2015 and 2023, the estimated overall RR per million donations declined for HBV (634.79 to 238.34) and HCV (1.69 to 0.99), but rose for HIV (4.08 to 11.40). Notably, the rising HIV RR-contrasting with the decline for HCV-was driven by increasing repeat-donor incidence and differences in the WP models used for risk estimation. CONCLUSION: While NAT-based screening was associated with low, declining RR for HBV and HCV, the rising HIV trajectory is concerning. This underscores the need for enhanced behavioural screening and donor selection strategies alongside molecular testing to address rising HIV risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Nucleic acid testing (NAT) has improved blood safety by shortening the diagnostic window period (WP) for transfusion-transmissible infections.
Design and population
Within the context of a national NAT-based screening programme, this study evaluated temporal trends for hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV) and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) among Turkish Red Crescent blood donors between 2015 and 2023.
Core methods
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 22,494,116 blood donations screened by serology and NAT. Prevalence, incidence and residual risk (RR) were estimated using standard incidence/WP models. Temporal trends were evaluated using linear regression, and NAT yield was assessed cumulatively.
Main results
The overall confirmed positivity (per 100,000 donors) was 288.5 for HBV, 16.2 for HCV and 14.8 for HIV. HBV and HCV showed significant downward trends (p < 0.01), while HIV rose significantly (p < 0.01). Cumulative NAT yields were 189.35, 2.94 and 2.15 per million donations for HBV, HCV and HIV, respectively. Between 2015 and 2023, the estimated overall RR per million donations declined for HBV (634.79 to 238.34) and HCV (1.69 to 0.99), but rose for HIV (4.08 to 11.40).
Interpretation and novelty
While NAT-based screening was associated with low, declining RR for HBV and HCV, the rising HIV trajectory is concerning. This underscores the need for enhanced behavioural screening and donor selection strategies alongside molecular testing to address rising HIV risks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform blood safety strategies by demonstrating the effectiveness of NAT in reducing residual risk for HBV and HCV, while highlighting the need for targeted interventions to address increasing HIV risk among blood donors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on mathematical models for residual risk estimation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the large sample size but is tempered by the abstract-only nature of the reported data.
