抗病毒药物 源自 FUBP1-IN-1 的新型化合物 0411 通过上调 DUSP5 对抗 乙型肝炎病毒 的活性

Antiviral activity of novel chemical compound 0411, derived from FUBP1-IN-1 against hepatitis B virus by upregulation of DUSP5
作者： Shuang Zhao, Haisha Wei, Yiyang Liang, Hui Fan
摘要中文翻译目前的一线药物很少能对慢性乙型肝炎患者实现功能性治愈,这促使我们探索新的抗HBV治疗策略。在这项研究中,我们发现新型化合物 0411(FUBP1 抑制剂 FUBPI-IN-1 的衍生物)在体外和体内均显着抑制 HBV 转录。转录组测序表明,0411 治疗 显着上调双特异性磷酸酶 5 (DUSP5) 基因的表达,该基因是 MAPK 途径的关键调节因子。进一步的功能研究表明,化合物0411的抗病毒药物活性依赖于DUSP5。从机制上讲,HepG2-NTCP 细胞中的 0411 治疗 显着增强了活性组蛋白修饰在 DUSP5 启动子上的沉积。总之,我们的研究结果证明了 0411 作为有效抗 HBV 转录药物的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前一线药物很少能达到慢性乙型肝炎患者的功能性治愈效果,这迫切需要探索新的抗HBV治疗策略。
设计与对象
该研究调查了新型化合物 0411(FUBP1-IN-1 的衍生物)在体外和体内针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的 乙型肝炎病毒 活性。
核心方法
转录组测序证明 0411 治疗 显着上调双特异性磷酸酶 5 (DUSP5) 基因的表达,该基因是 MAPK 途径的关键调节因子。
主要结果
新型化学化合物0411在体外和体内均显著抑制了HBV转录,且其抗病毒药物活性依赖于DUSP5。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,0411 通过增强活性组蛋白修饰在 DUSP5 启动子上的沉积,具有作为抗 HBV 转录的有效药物的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该研究通过上调DUSP5来靶向HBV转录,为慢性乙型肝炎患者提供了一种新的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内实验,还需要进一步的临床试验来验证化合物0411在人体中的有效性和安全性。

Antiviral activity of novel chemical compound 0411, derived from FUBP1-IN-1 against hepatitis B virus by upregulation of DUSP5

Authors: Shuang Zhao, Haisha Wei, Yiyang Liang, Hui Fan
Original abstract (English)
Current first-line drugs rarely achieve functional curesfor chronic hepatitis B patients, which urges us to explore novel anti-HBV therapeutic strategies. In this study, we found that the novel chemical compound 0411, a derivative of the FUBP1 inhibitor, FUBPI-IN-1, significantly suppressed HBV transcription both in vitro and in vivo. Transcriptome sequencing demonstrated that 0411 treatment markedly upregulated the expression of the dual-specificity phosphatase 5 (DUSP5) gene, a crucial regulator of the MAPK pathway. Further functional studies illustrated that the antiviral activity of compound 0411 was dependent on DUSP5. Mechanismly, 0411 treatment in HepG2-NTCP cells enhances the deposition of active histone modifications on the promoter of DUSP5 significantly. In conclusion, our findings demonstrate the potential of 0411 as an effective agent against HBV transcription.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current first-line drugs rarely achieve functional cures for chronic hepatitis B patients, which urges the exploration of novel anti-HBV therapeutic strategies.
Design and population
The study investigated the antiviral activity of the novel chemical compound 0411, a derivative of FUBP1-IN-1, against hepatitis B virus (HBV) both in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
Transcriptome sequencing was used to demonstrate that 0411 treatment markedly upregulated the expression of the dual-specificity phosphatase 5 (DUSP5) gene, a crucial regulator of the MAPK pathway.
Main results
The novel chemical compound 0411 significantly suppressed HBV transcription both in vitro and in vivo, and its antiviral activity was dependent on DUSP5.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the potential of 0411 as an effective agent against HBV transcription by enhancing the deposition of active histone modifications on the promoter of DUSP5.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a novel therapeutic strategy for chronic hepatitis B patients by targeting HBV transcription through the upregulation of DUSP5.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo experiments, and further clinical trials are needed to validate the efficacy and safety of compound 0411 in humans.

乙肝病毒感染缓解是 COVID-19 患者全身炎症反应增强的独立危险因素

Resolved HBV infection is an independent risk factor for enhanced systemic inflammatory responses in COVID-19 patients
作者： Yifan Xu, Jia Lu, Wei Chen, Xiaoxiang Yan, Weiguo Hu, Xiaogang Xiang, Lu Jia
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查与非 HBV 感染相比,已治愈的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的 COVID-19 患者的全身炎症反应和结果,以解决关于已治愈的 HBV 对 COVID-19 严重程度影响的知识差距。
设计与对象
这项单中心回顾性研究于2020年1月20日至2020年3月30日在中国武汉同济医院进行,涉及215例有可用检测结果的COVID-19病例。
核心方法
收集和分析人口统计学和临床​​特征以及包括炎症生物标志物和促炎细胞因子在内的实验室测试结果,以比较不同 HBV 感染组的结果。
主要结果
在215名患者中,15名患者当前感染乙肝病毒,134名患者已解决乙肝病毒感染,66名患者为非乙肝病毒感染;在已治愈的 HBV 组中,炎症生物标志物和促炎细胞因子显着升高,其中 59.7% 的患者发展为重症 COVID-19,而当前组和非 HBV 组中的这一比例分别为 46.7% 和 43.9%。
解释与创新
作者将已解决的 HBV 感染解释为 COVID-19 患者全身炎症反应增强和预后较差的独立危险因素,强调了已解决的病毒感染与 COVID-19 严重程度之间的新关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 监测 和已解决 HBV 感染的 COVID-19 患者的风险评估提供了信息,可能指导有针对性的监测和干预措施,以减轻严重后果。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和单中心设置限制了普遍性,尽管由于报告的明确关联和统计显着性,证据强度中等。

Resolved HBV infection is an independent risk factor for enhanced systemic inflammatory responses in COVID-19 patients

Authors: Yifan Xu, Jia Lu, Wei Chen, Xiaoxiang Yan, Weiguo Hu, Xiaogang Xiang, Lu Jia
Original abstract (English)
Purpose To investigate the systemic inflammatory responses and outcomes in coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) patients with resolved hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection or non-HBV infection. Methods This single-center, retrospective study was conducted in Tongji Hospital of Wuhan, China, from 20 January 2020 to 30 March 2020. A total of 215 COVID-19 cases with available test results were included. Demographic and clinical characteristics, and results of laboratory test results, were collected and analyzed. Results Of the 215 included patients, the numbers of patients with current HBV infection, resolved HBV infection, and non-HBV infection were 15, 134, and 66, respectively. Inflammatory biomarkers, such as C-reactive protein (CRP), procalcitonin (PCT), and ferritin, were significantly elevated in the resolved HBV infection group, as well pro-inflammatory cytokines, including interleukin-6 (IL-6), soluble interleukin-2 receptor (sIL-2R), tumor necrosis factor alpha (TNF- α ), and IL-8. Resolved HBV infection was independently associated with elevated proinflammatory cytokines. Patients with resolved HBV infection (59.7%) were more likely to develop into a severe state compared to patients with current HBV infection (46.7%) and non-HBV infection (43.9%). Conclusion Resolved HBV infection is an independent risk factor for patients with COVID-19. COVID-19 patients with resolved HBV infection are more likely to have enhanced systemic inflammatory responses and worse prognoses than those with non-HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate systemic inflammatory responses and outcomes in COVID-19 patients with resolved hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection compared to non-HBV infection, addressing a knowledge gap on the impact of resolved HBV on COVID-19 severity.
Design and population
This single-center, retrospective study was conducted at Tongji Hospital in Wuhan, China, from 20 January 2020 to 30 March 2020, involving 215 COVID-19 cases with available test results.
Core methods
Demographic and clinical characteristics, along with laboratory test results including inflammatory biomarkers and pro-inflammatory cytokines, were collected and analyzed to compare outcomes across HBV infection groups.
Main results
Of the 215 patients, 15 had current HBV infection, 134 had resolved HBV infection, and 66 had non-HBV infection; inflammatory biomarkers and pro-inflammatory cytokines were significantly elevated in the resolved HBV group, and 59.7% of these patients developed severe COVID-19 compared to 46.7% and 43.9% in current and non-HBV groups, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted resolved HBV infection as an independent risk factor for enhanced systemic inflammatory responses and worse prognoses in COVID-19 patients, highlighting a novel association between resolved viral infections and COVID-19 severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform surveillance and risk assessment for COVID-19 patients with resolved HBV infection, potentially guiding targeted monitoring and interventions to mitigate severe outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design and single-center setting limit generalizability, though the evidence strength is moderate due to the clear association and statistical significance reported.

一种针对慢性 HBV 感染的新型基于 LNP/mRNA 的 STING 免疫治疗方法 2334947

A novel LNP/mRNA-based STING immunotherapy approach for chronic HBV infection 2334947
作者： Jiapeng Wu, Lishan Su, James Ahodantin, Imran Khan, Rihe Liu
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒(HBV)感染仍然是全球主要的健康负担。目前的 抗病毒药物 疗法可抑制病毒复制,但无法消除 共价闭合环状DNA 或大幅减少 HBsAg,因此 功能性治愈 很少见。由于内源性表达低和激动剂递送效率低,肝细胞中 STING 通路的参与度较差,从而限制了 HBV 的先天免疫控制。我们开发了一种通用的 STING 模拟物 (uniSTING),通过编码 uniSTING mRNA 的脂质纳米颗粒传递,它驱动内膜独立的 STING 聚合物的形成,并优先激活 IRF3/I 型干扰素轴,同时最大限度地减少 NF-κB 信号传导。方法 用 LNP/uniSTING 处理 HBV 阳性细胞,然后评估 I 型干扰素和干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 的诱导。在体内,对 HBV 携带小鼠进行 LNP/uniSTING,并与 HBV 特异性先天性和适应性 免疫应答 一起监测血清 HBV 病毒血症。结果 uniSTING 在 HBV 阴性细胞中引发强烈的 IFN-I 反应。在 HepAD38 细胞系中,来自整合的 HBV 基因组 的 HBV 转录被多西环素抑制,主要从 HBV 共价闭合环状DNA 产生 HBV,uniSTING 诱导强的 IFN-β 和 ISG 表达,而在以高水平 HBV 复制为特征的 Dox 条件下,uniSTING 诱导的 IFN-β 和 ISG 反应减弱。在体内,对初始小鼠施用 LNP/uniSTING 诱导了强劲的 IFN-β 产生和 ISG 表达。在慢性 HBV 携带小鼠中,重复给予 LNP/uniSTING 导致血清 HBV DNA 和 HBsAg 水平持续降低,同时诱导抗 HBs IgG 反应。结论 这些研究结果表明,uniSTING 可以打破 HBV 免疫耐受并在体内引发功能性抗 HBs 抗体反应。总的来说,这项工作强调了 uniSTING 通过同时激活先天 抗病毒药物 通路和促进保护性体液免疫,作为慢性 HBV 感染的一种有前景的免疫治疗策略。资金来源 NIH R01 AI181664 主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的健康负担,目前的 抗病毒药物 疗法可抑制病毒复制,但无法消除 共价闭合环状DNA 或大幅降低 HBsAg,因此 功能性治愈 很少见。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种通用的 STING 模拟物 (uniSTING),通过编码 uniSTING mRNA 的脂质纳米颗粒传递,并在 HBV 阳性细胞和 HBV 携带小鼠体内进行了测试。
核心方法
用 LNP/uniSTING 处理 HBV 阳性细胞,然后评估 I 型干扰素和干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 诱导,并在体内对 HBV 携带小鼠施用 LNP/uniSTING,同时监测血清 HBV 病毒血症和 免疫应答。
主要结果
uniSTING 在 HBV 阴性细胞和初始小鼠中引发强烈的 IFN-I 反应,但在具有高水平 HBV 复制的 HepAD38 细胞系中,uniSTING 诱导的 IFN-β 和 ISG 反应减弱;在慢性 HBV 携带小鼠中,重复给予 LNP/uniSTING 导致血清 HBV DNA 和 HBsAg 水平持续降低,同时诱导抗 HBs IgG 反应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,uniSTING 可以打破 HBV 免疫耐受,并在体内引发功能性抗 HBs 抗体反应,强调它通过同时激活先天 抗病毒药物 通路和促进保护性体液免疫,成为慢性 HBV 感染的一种有前途的免疫治疗策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,基于 uniSTING 的免疫疗法可能通过减少病毒标记物和诱导保护性免疫来导致慢性 HBV 感染的 功能性治愈,为解决当前疗法的局限性提供了一种新方法。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的局限性,但该研究的优势得到了体外和体内模型的直接观察的支持,尽管证据仅限于摘要,可能不包括完整的方法学细节或长期随访。

A novel LNP/mRNA-based STING immunotherapy approach for chronic HBV infection 2334947

Authors: Jiapeng Wu, Lishan Su, James Ahodantin, Imran Khan, Rihe Liu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health burden. Current antiviral therapies suppress viral replication but fail to eliminate cccDNA or substantially reduce HBsAg, making functional cure rare. Innate immune control of HBV is limited by poor engagement of the STING pathway in hepatocytes due to low endogenous expression and inefficient agonist delivery. We developed a universal STING mimic (uniSTING) delivered via lipid nanoparticles encoding uniSTING mRNA, which drives formation of endomembrane-independent STING polymers and preferential activation of the IRF3/type I interferon axis while minimizing NF-κB signaling. Methods HBV-positive cells were treated with LNP/uniSTING, followed by assessment of type I interferon and interferon-stimulated genes (ISG) induction. In vivo, HBV carrier mice were administered LNP/uniSTING, and serum HBV viremia were monitored alongside HBV-specific innate and adaptive immune responses. Results uniSTING elicited strong IFN-I responses in HBV negative cells. In the HepAD38 cell line where HBV transcription from the integrated HBV genome is suppressed by doxycycline to produce HBV predominantly from HBV cccDNA, uniSTING induced robust IFN-β and ISGs expression, whereas in the Dox- condition, characterized by high-level HBV replication, uniSTING-induced IFN-β and ISG responses were attenuated. In vivo, administration of LNP/uniSTING to naive mice induced robust IFN-β production and ISG expression. In chronic HBV carrier mice, repeated administration of LNP/uniSTING resulted in sustained reductions in serum HBV DNA and HBsAg levels, accompanied by induction of anti-HBs IgG responses. Conclusion These findings demonstrate that uniSTING can break HBV immune tolerance and elicit a functional anti-HBs antibody response in vivo. Collectively, this work highlights uniSTING as a promising immunotherapeutic strategy for chronic HBV infection by simultaneously activating innate antiviral pathways and promoting protective humoral immunity. Funding Source NIH R01 AI181664 Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)

Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health burden, and current antiviral therapies suppress viral replication but fail to eliminate cccDNA or substantially reduce HBsAg, making functional cure rare.
Design and population
The study developed a universal STING mimic (uniSTING) delivered via lipid nanoparticles encoding uniSTING mRNA, which was tested in HBV-positive cells and in vivo in HBV carrier mice.
Core methods
HBV-positive cells were treated with LNP/uniSTING, followed by assessment of type I interferon and interferon-stimulated genes (ISG) induction, and in vivo, HBV carrier mice were administered LNP/uniSTING with monitoring of serum HBV viremia and immune responses.
Main results
uniSTING elicited strong IFN-I responses in HBV-negative cells and in naive mice, but in the HepAD38 cell line with high-level HBV replication, uniSTING-induced IFN-β and ISG responses were attenuated; in chronic HBV carrier mice, repeated administration of LNP/uniSTING resulted in sustained reductions in serum HBV DNA and HBsAg levels, accompanied by induction of anti-HBs IgG responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that uniSTING can break HBV immune tolerance and elicit a functional anti-HBs antibody response in vivo, highlighting it as a promising immunotherapeutic strategy for chronic HBV infection by simultaneously activating innate antiviral pathways and promoting protective humoral immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that uniSTING-based immunotherapy could potentially lead to functional cure in chronic HBV infection by reducing viral markers and inducing protective immunity, offering a novel approach to address the limitations of current therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations, but the study's strength is supported by direct observations in both in vitro and in vivo models, though the evidence is limited to the abstract and may not include full methodological details or long-term follow-up.

皮肤敲击技术对新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 疼痛的影响:一项随机对照试验

Effect of skin tap technique on pain during hepatitis B vaccination among newborns: a randomized controlled trial
作者： Chitra A., Vetriselvi P.
摘要中文翻译背景:疼痛是由刺激产生的不愉快的感觉。每个孩子对疼痛都有自己的看法。它是与实际或潜在的组织损伤相关的不愉快的感觉和情感体验。胶带带来的机械刺激可以减少微小的、带有疼痛的纤维的影响。本研究旨在评估皮肤敲击技术对乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间疼痛的影响。方法:对产后病房60例新生儿进行随机对照试验。采用连续抽样技术纳入符合纳入标准的新生儿。从医疗记录中收集有关新生儿和临床特征、出生顺序、孕龄、出生体重和分娩方式的数据。通过皮肤敲击技术收集有关使用新生儿疼痛量表评估乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间疼痛水平的数据。使用卡方检验、t 检验分析数据。结果:使用平均值和标准差对收集的数据进行分析。研究结果强调,在实验组30名通过皮肤敲击技术接种疫苗的新生儿中,大多数新生儿(20名(66.7%))出现轻度至中度疼痛,10名(33.3%)出现重度疼痛。对照组30名常规接种的新生儿中,大多数新生儿出现剧烈疼痛,分别为22名(73.3%)和8名(26.7%)轻至中度疼痛。与对照组相比,皮肤敲击技术是有效的。疼痛评分为3.83±1.147和5±1.174。分别具有统计显着性p=0.000。结论:皮肤敲击技术可有效减轻新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间的疼痛。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估皮肤敲击技术对乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间疼痛的影响,解决 疫苗接种 期间新生儿的疼痛感知问题。
设计与对象
采用连续抽样技术,对产后病房的 60 名新生儿进行了随机对照试验。
核心方法
使用新生儿疼痛量表收集有关新生儿临床特征和乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间疼痛水平的数据,并使用卡方检验、t 检验、平均值和标准差进行分析。
主要结果
使用皮肤敲击技术的实验组中的 30 名新生儿中,20 名(66.7%)有轻度至中度疼痛,10 名(33.3%)有重度疼痛,而对照组有 22 名(73.3%)重度疼痛,8 名(26.7%)有轻至中度疼痛(p=0.000)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,皮肤敲击技术可有效减轻新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间的疼痛,这表明各组之间存在统计学上的显着差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,实施皮肤敲击技术可以改善新生儿乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 期间的疼痛管理,可能提高 疫苗接种 的依从性和体验。
局限与证据强度
尽管随机对照设计和统计显着性结果提供了中等的证据强度,但该研究仅限于单一环境中 60 名新生儿的小样本量。

Effect of skin tap technique on pain during hepatitis B vaccination among newborns: a randomized controlled trial

Authors: Chitra A., Vetriselvi P.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Pain is all about an unpleasant sensation created by stimuli. Every child has his or her own perception of pain. It is unpleasant sensory and emotional experience associated with actual or potential tissue damage. Mechanical stimulation given by taping can diminish the influence of tiny, pain-carrying fibers. This study aimed to assess the effect of skin tap technique on pain during hepatitis-B vaccination. Methods: A randomized controlled trial was carried out among 60 newborns in postnatal ward. Consecutive sampling technique was used to enroll the newborn who met the inclusion criteria. Data pertaining to newborn and a clinical characteristics birth order, gestation age, birth weight and mode of delivery were collected from medical record. Data regarding assess the level of pain during hepatitis-B vaccination by using the neonatal pain scale were collected from skin tap technique. Data were analyzed using Chi-square test, t-test. Results: The data collected was analyzed by using mean and standard deviation. The study findings high light that among 30 newborns vaccinated with the skin tap technique in the experimental group, the majority of newborns, 20 (66.7%), had mild to moderate pain, and 10 (33.3%) had severe pain. Among 30 newborns vaccinated as a routine manner in the control group, the majority of newborns, 22 (73.3%), had severe pain, and 8 (26.7%) had mild to moderate pain, respectively. The skin tap technique was effective compared to the control group. The pain score was 3.83±1.147 and 5±1.174. Respectively with a statistically significant p=0.000. Conclusions: The skin tap technique was effective in reducing pain during hepatitis-B vaccination among the newborns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the effect of skin tap technique on pain during hepatitis-B vaccination, addressing the problem of pain perception in newborns during vaccination.
Design and population
A randomized controlled trial was carried out among 60 newborns in the postnatal ward using consecutive sampling technique.
Core methods
Data regarding newborn clinical characteristics and pain levels during hepatitis-B vaccination were collected using the neonatal pain scale and analyzed using Chi-square test, t-test, mean, and standard deviation.
Main results
Among 30 newborns in the experimental group using skin tap technique, 20 (66.7%) had mild to moderate pain and 10 (33.3%) had severe pain, compared to 22 (73.3%) with severe pain and 8 (26.7%) with mild to moderate pain in the control group (p=0.000).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the skin tap technique was effective in reducing pain during hepatitis-B vaccination among newborns, demonstrating a statistically significant difference between groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that implementing the skin tap technique could improve pain management during hepatitis-B vaccination in newborns, potentially enhancing vaccination compliance and experience.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small sample size of 60 newborns in a single setting, though the randomized controlled design and statistically significant results provide moderate evidence strength.

慢性乙型肝炎 中贝匹罗韦森基于价值的价格走廊:成本压缩环境中的早期卫生技术评估

Value-based price corridor for bepirovirsen in chronic hepatitis B: an early health technology assessment in a cost-compressed setting
作者： Kaijie Yao, Mengxia Yan, Yun Bao, Ying Chen, Wen Li, Bin Wu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在估计贝匹罗韦森(慢性乙型肝炎 的一种研究性有限疗程疗法)的基于价值的价格走廊,在低成本通用核苷(酸)类似物创建严格的比较环境的情况下。
设计与对象
使用终生马尔可夫模型进行了早期卫生技术评估 (eHTA),该模型模拟贝匹罗韦生 300 毫克/周,持续 24 周,加上背景核苷类似物,与中国特定的比较标准护理方案进行比较。
核心方法
该模型使用以 2024 年人民币计值的中国成本投入,转换为美元,并应用每个质量调整生命年 (QALY) 13,500 美元的成本效益阈值,并对更高阈值和维持反应情景进行敏感性分析。
主要结果
与恩替卡韦相比,基本案例产生了 0.82 个额外的 QAL,按照基于价值的价格,每个患者的增量总成本为 10,833.72 美元,避免了每 10,000 名患者中 906 例肝细胞癌病例。
解释与创新
作者解释说,贝匹罗韦森的基于价值的价格取决于比较药物价格、成本效益阈值选择、乙型肝炎表面抗原血清清除的持久性、安全性、监测需求和治愈状态效用假设。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为成本压缩环境中新型乙型肝炎疗法的定价策略提供信息,并强调针对功能性治愈策略进行特定环境价值评估的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖于 II 期疗效输入、不确定的长期复发率以及无法可靠地估计不良事件和监测成本,证据强度中等。

Value-based price corridor for bepirovirsen in chronic hepatitis B: an early health technology assessment in a cost-compressed setting

Authors: Kaijie Yao, Mengxia Yan, Yun Bao, Ying Chen, Wen Li, Bin Wu
Original abstract (English)
We conducted an early health technology assessment (eHTA) of bepirovirsen, an investigational finite-course therapy for chronic hepatitis B. In China, low-cost generic nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) create a demanding comparator environment. The value-based price of a potential functional-cure strategy therefore depends on local comparator prices, threshold selection, cure durability, safety, monitoring requirements, and cured-state utility assumptions. A lifetime Markov model simulated bepirovirsen 300 mg/week for 24 weeks plus background NAs against comparator-specific standard-of-care options. Chinese cost inputs were valued in 2024 renminbi (RMB) and converted to US dollars using the 2024 annual average exchange rate of 7.1957 RMB per US dollar. The base case used a China-context cost-effectiveness threshold (CET) of $13,500 per quality-adjusted life-year (QALY), approximating China’s 2024 gross domestic product (GDP) per capita after currency conversion, and compared it with higher threshold scenarios. Because reliable long-term relapse rates were unavailable, a 48-week maintained-response scenario set bepirovirsen efficacy to 9.0%, corresponding to the B-Clear primary-outcome rate among participants receiving background NA therapy. Bepirovirsen yielded 0.41–0.82 incremental QALYs across standard-of-care comparators. Compared with entecavir (ETV), the base case generated 0.82 additional QALYs, incurred an incremental total cost of $10,833.72 per patient at the value-based price, and avoided 906 hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) cases per 10,000 patients. Under the $13,500/QALY threshold, the value-based price per dose ranged from $92.92 to $367.44 across comparators. In the 48-week maintained-response scenario, the value-based price per dose was $70.29 versus ETV and ranged from $28.62 to $294.24 across comparators. The societal-perspective scenario increased the value-based price per dose to $426.41. Model-cohort expected value of perfect information (EVPI) was $0.46 million at the base-case threshold. Bepirovirsen’s value-based price is conditional on comparator prices, CET selection, durability of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance, safety, monitoring needs, and cured-state utility assumptions. These eHTA results are hypothesis-generating and should be interpreted within the limits of phase II-derived efficacy inputs, uncertain relapse pathways, and adverse-event and monitoring costs that cannot yet be estimated reliably. This early health technology assessment (eHTA) estimates a value-based price corridor for bepirovirsen, a finite-course investigational therapy for chronic hepatitis B, in a setting where low-cost generic nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) create a stringent comparator. At a $13,500 per quality-adjusted life-year (QALY) threshold, the model estimated a value-based price of $92.92-$367.44 per dose across standard-of-care comparators. Compared with entecavir (ETV), the base case produced 0.82 additional QALYs and an incremental total cost of $10,833.72 per patient at the value-based price. In the 48-week maintained-response scenario, the corresponding value-based price versus ETV was $70.29 per dose. Scenario analyses show that the corridor remains sensitive to maintained response, cured-state utility, societal-cost assumptions, and the selected cost-effectiveness threshold.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to estimate a value-based price corridor for bepirovirsen, an investigational finite-course therapy for chronic hepatitis B, in a setting where low-cost generic nucleos(t)ide analogues create a stringent comparator environment.
Design and population
An early health technology assessment (eHTA) was conducted using a lifetime Markov model that simulated bepirovirsen 300 mg/week for 24 weeks plus background nucleos(t)ide analogues against comparator-specific standard-of-care options in China.
Core methods
The model used Chinese cost inputs valued in 2024 renminbi, converted to US dollars, and applied a cost-effectiveness threshold of $13,500 per quality-adjusted life-year (QALY), with sensitivity analyses for higher thresholds and maintained-response scenarios.
Main results
Compared with entecavir, the base case generated 0.82 additional QALs and an incremental total cost of $10,833.72 per patient at the value-based price, avoiding 906 hepatocellular carcinoma cases per 10,000 patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that bepirovirsen's value-based price is conditional on comparator prices, cost-effectiveness threshold selection, durability of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance, safety, monitoring needs, and cured-state utility assumptions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform pricing strategies for novel hepatitis B therapies in cost-compressed settings and highlight the importance of context-specific value assessments for functional-cure strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on phase II-derived efficacy inputs, uncertain long-term relapse rates, and inability to reliably estimate adverse-event and monitoring costs.

开发和验证机器学习衍生的双指标工具,用于识别 乙型肝炎病毒 相关的慢加急性肝衰竭

Development and validation of a machine learning-derived dual-indicator tool for identification of hepatitis B virus-related pre-acute-on-chronic liver failure
作者： Tianyi Zhang, Shaoli You, Jinjin Luo, Jun Ling, Sa Lv, Yiwen Xu, Shaojie Xin, Xiaohe Xiao, Jun Li, Bing Zhu
摘要中文翻译背景与目标 入院时不符合慢性急性肝衰竭 (ACLF) 标准的患者仍面临疾病进展和 死亡率 的高风险。本研究旨在开发和验证机器学习衍生的临床工具,用于早期识别 HBV-pre-ACLF。方法 我们分析了 1,682 名经历 HBV 相关慢性肝病急性恶化但无 ACLF 的患者。通过随机森林和 SHAP 分析,我们确定了 7 天窗口内 ACLF 发病的关键预测因子。然后,我们在 260 名患者的 队列研究 测试中验证了诊断阈值。结果 总胆红素 (TBIL) 和 INR 被确定为 ACLF 进展最关键的预测因子。我们建立了最佳诊断阈值 TBIL ≥131.5 μmol/L 和 INR ≥1.35。制定了两个连续标准:用于高敏感度筛查的 ACLF-O 前(满足任一阈值)和用于风险确认的 ACLF-A 前(满足两个阈值)。在推导 队列研究 中,ACLF-O 前的敏感性达到 98.3%,而 ACLF-A 前的预测阳性预测值为 33.09%。这些结果在 队列研究 测试中成功得到验证,其中 pre-ACLF-O 保持 100% 的敏感性,pre-ACLF-A 显示出 37.04% 的阳性预测值。结论 这种双指标工具可以轻松有效地识别 HBV-pre-ACLF。依次使用这些标准为早期风险分层提供了实用的策略,使临床医生能够对高危患者及时采取干预措施。

问题与背景
背景与目标 入院时不符合慢性急性肝衰竭 (ACLF) 标准的患者仍面临疾病进展和 死亡率 的高风险。
设计与对象
本研究旨在开发和验证机器学习衍生的临床工具,用于早期识别 HBV-pre-ACLF。我们分析了 1,682 名经历 HBV 相关慢性肝病急性恶化但没有 ACLF 的患者。
核心方法
通过随机森林和 SHAP 分析,我们确定了 7 天窗口内 ACLF 发病的关键预测因子。
主要结果
结果 总胆红素 (TBIL) 和 INR 被确定为 ACLF 进展最关键的预测因子。这些结果在 队列研究 测试中成功得到验证,其中 pre-ACLF-O 保持 100% 的敏感性,pre-ACLF-A 显示出 37.04% 的阳性预测值。
解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
它可以为 HBV 相关肝病进展的靶向 监测 和诊断方案的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。该研究基于推导和测试 队列研究,其结果在单独的测试 队列研究 中得到验证,表明证据强度中等。

Development and validation of a machine learning-derived dual-indicator tool for identification of hepatitis B virus-related pre-acute-on-chronic liver failure

Authors: Tianyi Zhang, Shaoli You, Jinjin Luo, Jun Ling, Sa Lv, Yiwen Xu, Shaojie Xin, Xiaohe Xiao, Jun Li, Bing Zhu
Original abstract (English)
Background & aims Patients who do not meet the criteria for Acute-on-Chronic Liver Failure (ACLF) at admission still face a high risk of disease progression and mortality. This study aimed to develop and validate a machine learning-derived clinical tool for the early identification of HBV-pre-ACLF. Methods We analyzed 1,682 patients experiencing acute deterioration of HBV-related chronic liver disease but without ACLF. By random forest and SHAP analysis, we identified key predictors of ACLF onset within a 7-day window. We then validated the diagnostic thresholds in a test cohort of 260 patients. Results Total bilirubin (TBIL) and INR were identified as the most critical predictors of ACLF progression. We established optimal diagnostic thresholds at TBIL ≥131.5 μmol/L and INR ≥1.35. Two sequential criteria were developed: pre-ACLF-O (meeting either threshold) for highly sensitive screening, and pre-ACLF-A (meeting both thresholds) for risk confirmation. In the derivation cohort, pre-ACLF-O achieved 98.3% sensitivity, while pre-ACLF-A predicted a 33.09% positive predictive value. These results were successfully validated in the test cohort, where pre-ACLF-O maintained 100% sensitivity and pre-ACLF-A demonstrated a 37.04% positive predictive value. Conclusions This dual-indicator tool easily and effectively identifies HBV-pre-ACLF. Using these criteria sequentially provides a practical strategy for early risk stratification, allowing clinicians to initiate timely interventions for high-risk patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background & aims Patients who do not meet the criteria for Acute-on-Chronic Liver Failure (ACLF) at admission still face a high risk of disease progression and mortality.
Design and population
This study aimed to develop and validate a machine learning-derived clinical tool for the early identification of HBV-pre-ACLF. We analyzed 1,682 patients experiencing acute deterioration of HBV-related chronic liver disease but without ACLF.
Core methods
By random forest and SHAP analysis, we identified key predictors of ACLF onset within a 7-day window.
Main results
Results Total bilirubin (TBIL) and INR were identified as the most critical predictors of ACLF progression. These results were successfully validated in the test cohort, where pre-ACLF-O maintained 100% sensitivity and pre-ACLF-A demonstrated a 37.04% positive predictive value.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions This dual-indicator tool easily and effectively identifies HBV-pre-ACLF. Using these criteria sequentially provides a practical strategy for early risk stratification, allowing clinicians to initiate timely interventions for high-risk patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
It can inform the development of targeted surveillance and diagnostic protocols for HBV-related liver disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The study is based on a derivation and test cohort, with results validated in a separate test cohort, suggesting moderate evidence strength.

新型 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 细胞因子调节 乙型肝炎病毒 X 蛋白 (HBx) 的动态核和细胞质定位

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx)
作者： Yasuo Ariumi
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。鉴定了几种新型 HBx 相互作用 宿主 细胞因子:DDX1、DDX3、MOV10 RNA 解旋酶、INI1/hSNF5 染色质重塑因子、Rad18 DNA 修复酶和 CPSF6 裂解因子。DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。相反,INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。重要的是,DDX3、MOV10 和 INI1/hSNF5 限制 HBV 复制,而 CPSF6 促进 HBV 复制。此外,DDX1 增强 HBx 介导的 NF-κB 转录,而 MOV10 和 Rad18 抑制它。总之,HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在鉴定与 HBV X 蛋白 (HBx) 相互作用的 宿主 细胞因子,并了解它们在调节 HBx 定位和病毒复制中的作用。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。
设计与对象
该研究是在实验室环境中使用人类乙型肝炎病毒感染模型进行的。它涉及通过生化和细胞测定来鉴定与 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子。
核心方法
研究人员使用生化和细胞测定来识别 HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子,包括细胞质灶或聚集体和核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体的共定位研究。他们还评估了这些因素对 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录的影响。
主要结果
DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、IGC 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HBx 相互作用的 宿主 因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。这项研究确定了几种与 HBx 相互作用并对病毒过程具有独特影响的新因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 HBV 复制和 发病机制 的分子机制的见解,这可以为靶向治疗的开发提供信息并提高对 HBV 相关肝癌发生的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,不提供详细的统计分析或实验对照。由于缺乏有关这些因素体内功能影响的全面数据,证据强度中等。

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx)

Authors: Yasuo Ariumi
Original abstract (English)
The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis. Several novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors: DDX1, DDX3, MOV10 RNA helicases, INI1/hSNF5 chromatin-remodeling factor, Rad18 DNA repair enzyme, and CPSF6 cleavage factor were identified. DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. In contrast, INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli. Importantly, DDX3, MOV10, and INI1/hSNF5 restricted HBV replication, whereas CPSF6 facilitated HBV replication. Furthermore, DDX1 enhanced HBx-mediated NF-κB transcription, whereas MOV10 and Rad18 inhibited it. Altogether, HBx-interacting host factors determine dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify host cellular factors that interact with HBV X protein (HBx) and understand their role in regulating HBx localization and viral replication. The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a laboratory setting using human HBV infection models. It involved the identification of host factors interacting with HBx through biochemical and cellular assays.
Core methods
The researchers used biochemical and cellular assays to identify HBx-interacting host factors, including colocalization studies in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates and nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. They also assessed the impact of these factors on HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.
Main results
DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, IGCs, and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HBx-interacting host factors determine the dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription. This study identified several novel factors that interact with HBx and have distinct effects on viral processes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HBV replication and pathogenesis, which could inform the development of targeted therapies and improve understanding of HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence and does not provide detailed statistical analyses or experimental controls. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of comprehensive data on the functional impact of these factors in vivo.

Hubungan Kadar 白蛋白 Plasma pada Penderita TB Mdr dengan Ko-Infeksi 乙型肝炎

Hubungan Kadar Albumin Plasma pada Penderita TB Mdr dengan Ko-Infeksi Hepatitis B
作者： Farah Alfarisi, Fauna Herawati, Umi Fatmawati
摘要中文翻译【摘要】:结核病是由结核分枝杆菌引起的一种慢性传染病。结核分枝杆菌 (Mtb) 病菌的耐药性是由染色体中自发的 突变 引起的。在从未接受过OAT的患者中,经历过突变(野生型耐药突变体)的Mtb病菌比例非常小。TB 治疗 会选择性抑制 Mtb 菌群,导致敏感 Mtb 菌群被杀死,而突变菌群则会繁殖并产生对 OAT 的抗性(获得性抗性)。结核病患者常见的合并感染是乙型肝炎。乙型肝炎感染是一种肝脏疾病,会引起炎症和肝细胞损伤。结核病患者中 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染率为 0.5% 至 44%。低白蛋白血症通常与结核病和乙型肝炎有关。乙型肝炎常见于乙型肝炎病毒感染引起的肝损伤,而在结核病中,低白蛋白血症通常与结核病患者的营养不良和营养摄入不足有关。这一假设与患者目前的情况相符,实验室结果显示,HBsAg 值为 1000,证实患者被诊断为乙型肝炎。此外,患者还患有耐多药结核病,且营养摄入不足,患者的 BMI 仅 19.53。关键词:乙型肝炎,低蛋白血症,合并感染,多重耐药,结核,多重耐药。摘要 结核病与结核分枝杆菌有关。结核分枝杆菌 (Mtb) 会产生耐药性。Proporsi kuman Mtb yang sudah mengalami mutasi(野生型抗性突变体) pada pasien yang tidak pernah mendapatkan OAT sangat sedikit。Pengobatan TB menyebabkan hambatan selektif pada populasi kuman Mtb sehingga kuman Mtb sensitif dibunuh,sementara populasi mutan akan bereproduksi dan menyebabkan terjadinya revansi terhadap OAT (resistansi didapat). Ko-infeksi yang sering terjadi pada pasien TB adalah B 型肝炎。Infeksi 乙型肝炎 adalah penyakit hati yang menyebabkan peradangan dan kerusakan hepatosit。Infeksi 病毒乙型肝炎 (HBV) kronis pada pasien 结核病 berkisar antara 0,5% hingga 44%。高白蛋白血症 乙型肝炎 乙型肝炎 乙型肝炎 乙型肝炎 乙型肝炎 乙型肝炎 乙型肝炎 结核病 乙型肝炎 结核病结核病。诊断乙型肝炎、诊断乙型肝炎、检测 HBsAg 1000、诊断结核病 MDR、诊断 BMI 诊断哈尼亚 19.53。Kata Kunci:乙型肝炎、低蛋白血症、Ko-Infeksi、多重耐药、结核病、多重耐药。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨耐多药结核病(MDR TB)患者血浆白蛋白水平与合并感染乙型肝炎之间的关系。
设计与对象
该研究检查了一名被诊断为乙型肝炎的患者,实验室结果显示 HBsAg 值为 1000,且患有耐多药结核病,且营养摄入不足,BMI 仅 19.53。
核心方法
该研究假设低白蛋白血症与结核病和乙型肝炎有关,乙型肝炎会导致肝损伤,而结核病则与营养不良和营养摄入不足有关。
主要结果
患者的病情与假设相符,实验室结果证实患有乙型肝炎,并且患有耐多药结核病且营养摄入不足,BMI 为 19.53。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,合并感染患者的低白蛋白血症可能是由乙型肝炎引起的肝损伤和结核病相关营养不良造成的。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了监测合并乙型肝炎的耐多药结核病患者的白蛋白水平对于评估营养状况和肝功能的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于单个病例研究,限制了普遍性,但提供了对低白蛋白血症、结核病和乙型肝炎合并感染之间关系的具体见解。

Hubungan Kadar Albumin Plasma pada Penderita TB Mdr dengan Ko-Infeksi Hepatitis B

Authors: Farah Alfarisi, Fauna Herawati, Umi Fatmawati
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Tuberculosis is a chronic infectious disease caused by the bacterium Mycobacterium tuberculosis. Resistance of the Mycobacterium tuberculosis (Mtb) germ is caused by spontaneous mutations in the chromosome. The proportion of Mtb germs that have undergone mutation (wild-type resistant mutants) in patients who have never received OAT is very small. TB treatment causes selective inhibition of the Mtb germ population, leading to the killing of sensitive Mtb germs, while the mutant population will reproduce and cause resistance to OAT (acquired resistance). A common co-infection in TB patients is Hepatitis B. Hepatitis B infection is a liver disease that causes inflammation and damage to hepatocytes. Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in tuberculosis patients ranges from 0.5% to 44%. Hypoalbuminemia is often associated with both TB and Hepatitis B. It frequently occurs in hepatitis B due to liver damage caused by hepatitis B virus infection, while in TB, hypoalbuminemia is often linked to malnutrition and poor nutritional intake in TB patients. This hypothesis aligns with the patient's current condition, where the patient is diagnosed with hepatitis B confirmed by laboratory results showing HBsAg values 1000. Additionally, the patient also suffers from MDR TB with poor nutritional intake, as evidenced by the patient's BMI of only 19.53. Keywords: Hepatitis B, Hypoalbuminemia, Co-infection, Multi-drug resistant, Tuberculosis, Multi-Drug Resistant. ABSTRAK Tuberkulosis ada suatu penyakit kronik menular yang disebabkan oleh bakteri Mycobacterium tuberculosis. Resistansi kuman Mycobacterium tuberculosis (Mtb) disebabkan oleh mutasi spontan pada kromosom. Proporsi kuman Mtb yang sudah mengalami mutasi (wild-type resistant mutants) pada pasien yang tidak pernah mendapatkan OAT sangat sedikit. Pengobatan TB menyebabkan hambatan selektif pada populasi kuman Mtb sehingga kuman Mtb sensitif dibunuh, sementara populasi mutan akan bereproduksi dan menyebabkan terjadinya resistansi terhadap OAT (resistansi didapat). Ko-infeksi yang sering terjadi pada pasien TB adalah Hepatitis B. Infeksi hepatitis B adalah penyakit hati yang menyebabkan peradangan dan kerusakan hepatosit. Infeksi virus hepatitis B (HBV) kronis pada pasien tuberkulosis berkisar antara 0,5% hingga 44%. Hipoalbuminemia sering dikaitkan dengan penyakit TB maupun Hepatitis B. Pada hepatitis B sering terjadi dikarenakan adanya kerusakan hati yang disebabkan oleh infeksi virus hepatitis B, sedangkan pada TB sering terjadi hipoalbumin dikaitkan dengan malnutrisi dan kurangnya asupan gizi pada pasien TB. Hipotesis ini sesuai dengan kondisi pasien pada saat ini, dimana pasien didiagnosis hepatitis B yang ditegakkan dengan hasil pemeriksaan labortarorium dimana nilai HBsAg 1000, selain itu pasien juga menderita TB MDR dengan asupan gizi yang buruk, dimana dapat dilihat BMI pasien hanya 19.53. Kata Kunci: Hepatitis B, Hipoalbuminemia, Ko-Infeksi, Multi Drug Resistant, Tuberkulosis, Multi Drug Resistant.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between plasma albumin levels in patients with multidrug-resistant tuberculosis (MDR TB) and co-infection with hepatitis B.
Design and population
The study examined a patient diagnosed with hepatitis B confirmed by laboratory results showing HBsAg values of 1000 and MDR TB with poor nutritional intake, evidenced by a BMI of only 19.53.
Core methods
The study hypothesized that hypoalbuminemia is associated with both TB and hepatitis B, with hepatitis B causing liver damage and TB linked to malnutrition and poor nutritional intake.
Main results
The patient's condition aligned with the hypothesis, showing hepatitis B confirmed by laboratory results and MDR TB with poor nutritional intake, evidenced by a BMI of 19.53.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that hypoalbuminemia in co-infected patients may result from both liver damage due to hepatitis B and malnutrition associated with TB.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of monitoring albumin levels in MDR TB patients with hepatitis B co-infection to assess nutritional status and liver function.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single case study, limiting generalizability, but provides specific insights into the relationship between hypoalbuminemia, TB, and hepatitis B co-infection.

针对 慢性乙型肝炎 感染的基于 T 细胞的免疫疗法的开发 2309924

Development of T-cell-based immunotherapy against chronic Hepatitis B infection 2309924
作者： Sofiya Yusova, Benjamin Bimber, Sreya Biswas, Benjamin Burwitz, Brayden Hinrichs, Savannah Lutz, Jason Reed, Lauren Rust, Jenni Tran
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 尽管已知 T 细胞对于 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 清除至关重要,但目前尚无 FDA 批准的针对慢性 HBV 感染的免疫疗法,慢性 HBV 感染是全球肝癌、肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因之一。我们的目的是在急性 HBV 清除的恒河猴 (RM) 模型中表征和测试 HBV 特异性 T 细胞受体 (TCR)。方法 我们使用来自 RM 的非实质肝细胞(通过酶联免疫斑点和细胞内细胞因子染色 (ICS) 清除 HBV)来分析 HBV 特异性 T 细胞反应。我们使用 TNF-α 加工抑制剂 (TAPI-0) 对来自反应最强的动物的激活的 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞进行活体分选,并通过单细胞 RNAseq 分析其转录谱以及 T 细胞受体 (TCR)。我们使用逆转录病毒方法表达初始 CD8+ T 细胞中前 8 个最富集的 TCR 的 α 和 β TCR 链,以确认其特异性、绘制最小表位图并识别 MHC 限制。结果我们鉴定出三只动物对相同的 HBV 表面 15 聚体具有强烈的 CD8+ T 细胞反应。转录谱显示,响应的 CD8+ T 细胞主要表达 αβ TCR 链,并且高度激活。使用 ICS,我们确认了 3 个 TCR 的抗病毒活性。使用转导这些 TCR 的 T 细胞进行的肽稀释实验表明,免疫原性肽是 FLLTRILTI,已知它由人类 HLA-A*02 呈递。我们还确定,在 RM 中,该肽的限制性等位基因是 Mamu-B*031:01:01。结论 开发针对慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的免疫疗法的主要挑战之一是缺乏临床前动物模型。我们证明 RM 可用于识别 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应。未来的方向包括开发 MHC 肽四聚体来检测和阐明 HBV 特异性 T 细胞反应,识别针对其他 HBV 蛋白的 HBV 特异性 TCR,以及测试基于 T 细胞的免疫治疗的效率和安全性。资金来源 不适用 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 T 细胞在病毒清除中发挥重要作用,但 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球肝细胞癌的一个主要原因,目前尚无 FDA 批准的免疫疗法。该研究解决了缺乏用于开发乙型肝炎特异性免疫疗法的临床前动物模型的问题。
设计与对象
该研究在急性 HBV 清除的恒河猴 (RM) 模型中表征并测试了 HBV 特异性 T 细胞受体 (TCR)。对来自清除 HBV 的 RM 的非实质肝细胞进行分析,以确定 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应。
核心方法
使用酶联免疫斑点和细胞内细胞因子染色 (ICS) 对非实质肝细胞分析 HBV 特异性 T 细胞反应。对激活的 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞进行活体分选,并通过单细胞 RNA 测序分析其转录谱和 TCR。前 8 个富集的 TCR 在初始 CD8+ T 细胞中表达,以确认特异性、绘制最小表位图并识别 MHC 限制。
主要结果
三只动物对相同的 HBV 表面 15 聚体表现出强烈的 CD8+ T 细胞反应。响应的 CD8+ T 细胞主要表达 αβ TCR 链并且高度激活。三个 TCR 通过 ICS 证实了抗病毒活性,肽稀释实验鉴定出免疫原性肽为 FLLTRILTI,由人类中的 HLA-A*02 和 RM 中的 Mamu-B*031:01:01 呈递。
解释与创新
该研究表明,恒河猴可以作为识别 HBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应的临床前模型,解决免疫疗法开发中的关键挑战。新颖之处在于该模型中免疫原性肽和 MHC 限制的特征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用恒河猴作为评估 HBV 特异性 T 细胞免疫疗法的模型,有可能加速慢性 HBV 治疗 的转化研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据基础,缺乏详细的统计分析,并且不报告临床结果或安全性数据。由于研究结果的初步性质,证据强度被评估为中等。

Development of T-cell-based immunotherapy against chronic Hepatitis B infection 2309924

Authors: Sofiya Yusova, Benjamin Bimber, Sreya Biswas, Benjamin Burwitz, Brayden Hinrichs, Savannah Lutz, Jason Reed, Lauren Rust, Jenni Tran
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Even though T cells are known to be essential for Hepatitis B virus (HBV) clearance, there is currently no FDA-approved immunotherapy against chronic HBV infection, one of the major global causes of liver cancer, hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). We aimed to characterize and test HBV-specific T cell receptors (TCRs) in a rhesus macaque (RMs) model of acute HBV clearance. Methods We analyzed HBV-specific T cell responses using non-parenchymal liver cells from RMs that cleared HBV by enzyme-linked immunospot and intracellular cytokine staining (ICS). We live-sorted activated HBV-specific CD8+ T cells from an animal with the strongest response using the TNF-α processing inhibitor (TAPI-0) and analyzed their transcriptional profile as well as T cell receptors (TCRs) via single-cell RNAseq. We used the retroviral method of expressing the α and β TCR chains from the top 8 most enriched TCRs in naive CD8+ T cells to confirm their specificity, map the minimal epitope, and identify MHC restriction. Results We identified three animals with strong CD8+ T cell responses against the same HBV surface 15-mer. The transcriptional profile showed that the responding CD8+ T cells primarily expressed αβ TCR chains and were highly activated. Using ICS, we confirmed the anti-viral activity of 3 TCRs. Peptide dilution experiments using T cells transduced with these TCRs showed that the immunogenic peptide is FLLTRILTI, which is known to be presented by HLA-A*02 in humans. We also determined that in RMs the restricting allele for this peptide is Mamu-B*031:01:01. Conclusion One of the main challenges in developing immunotherapy against chronic HBV infection is a lack of pre-clinical animal models. We show that RMs can be used to identify HBV-specific CD8+ T cell responses. Future directions include developing MHC-peptide tetramers to detect and elucidate HBV-specific T cell responses, identifying HBV-specific TCRs against other HBV proteins, and testing T-cell-based immunotherapy efficiency and safety. Funding Source n/a Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)

Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global cause of hepatocellular carcinoma with no FDA-approved immunotherapy despite the essential role of T cells in viral clearance. The study addressed the lack of pre-clinical animal models for developing HBV-specific immunotherapies.
Design and population
The study characterized and tested HBV-specific T cell receptors (TCRs) in a rhesus macaque (RMs) model of acute HBV clearance. Non-parenchymal liver cells from RMs that cleared HBV were analyzed to identify HBV-specific CD8+ T cell responses.
Core methods
HBV-specific T cell responses were analyzed using enzyme-linked immunospot and intracellular cytokine staining (ICS) on non-parenchymal liver cells. Activated HBV-specific CD8+ T cells were live-sorted and their transcriptional profiles and TCRs were analyzed via single-cell RNA sequencing. The top 8 enriched TCRs were expressed in naive CD8+ T cells to confirm specificity, map the minimal epitope, and identify MHC restriction.
Main results
Three animals exhibited strong CD8+ T cell responses against the same HBV surface 15-mer. The responding CD8+ T cells primarily expressed αβ TCR chains and were highly activated. Three TCRs confirmed anti-viral activity via ICS, and peptide dilution experiments identified the immunogenic peptide as FLLTRILTI, presented by HLA-A*02 in humans and Mamu-B*031:01:01 in RMs.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that rhesus macaques can serve as a pre-clinical model for identifying HBV-specific CD8+ T cell responses, addressing a key challenge in immunotherapy development. Novelty lies in characterizing immunogenic peptides and MHC restriction in this model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of rhesus macaques as a model for evaluating HBV-specific T cell-based immunotherapies, potentially accelerating translational research for chronic HBV treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only evidence base, lacks detailed statistical analyses, and does not report clinical outcomes or safety data. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the preliminary nature of the findings.

ddOTs:一种多重定量 ddPCR 方法,用于解析重叠的 乙型肝炎病毒 转录本以破译 共价闭合环状DNA 驱动的转录

ddOTs: A multiplexed quantitative ddPCR approach for resolving overlapping hepatitis B virus transcripts to decipher cccDNA-driven transcription
作者： Nazim Sarica, Oceane Lopez, João Diogo Dias, Victor Boschetti, Irene Meki, Basile Jay, Gael Petitjean, David Durantel, Christine Neuveut
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染持续成为全球健康危机,导致肝细胞癌等危及生命的肝脏病变。HBV 恢复能力的核心是共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA),这是一种病毒微型染色体,可以协调病毒转录并维持感染。由于病毒开放阅读框的密集重叠,剖析 共价闭合环状DNA 驱动的转录仍然是一项艰巨的挑战,这会产生具有共享序列的 RNA 转录本,使精确定量变得复杂,并阻碍了阐明 HBV 转录调控的努力。为了解决这个瓶颈,我们开发了一种名为 ddPCR for Overlapping Transcripts (ddOTs) 的多重检测,能够同时定量所有主要 HBV RNA 种类,包括剪接变体,具有前所未有的特异性和灵敏度。该方法通过利用 ddPCR 的高分辨率能力来解卷积重叠转录本,从而克服了当前的局限性,从而能够直接询问单个启动子/增强子活性和 RNA 稳定性。通过为稀有生物样品中的精确 RNA 分析提供经济高效、可扩展的解决方案,这一突破性工具为探索 共价闭合环状DNA 生物学和加速 抗病毒药物 药物开发开辟了新途径。

设计与对象
通过为稀有生物样品中的精确 RNA 分析提供经济高效、可扩展的解决方案,这一突破性工具为探索 共价闭合环状DNA 生物学和加速 抗病毒药物 药物开发开辟了新途径。

ddOTs: A multiplexed quantitative ddPCR approach for resolving overlapping hepatitis B virus transcripts to decipher cccDNA-driven transcription

Authors: Nazim Sarica, Oceane Lopez, João Diogo Dias, Victor Boschetti, Irene Meki, Basile Jay, Gael Petitjean, David Durantel, Christine Neuveut
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B virus infection persists as a global health crisis, driving life-threatening liver pathologies such as hepatocellular carcinoma. Central to HBV’s resilience is the covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA), a viral minichromosome that orchestrates viral transcription and sustains infection. Dissecting cccDNA-driven transcription remains a formidable challenge due to the dense overlap of viral open reading frames, which generates RNA transcripts with shared sequences, complicating precise quantification and hindering efforts to unravel HBV’s transcriptional regulation. To address this bottleneck, we developed a multiplexed assay named ddPCR for Overlapping Transcripts (ddOTs) capable of simultaneously quantifying all major HBV RNA species, including splice variants, with unprecedented specificity and sensitivity. This method overcomes current limitations by leveraging the high-resolution power of ddPCR to deconvolute overlapping transcripts, enabling direct interrogation of individual promoter/enhancer activities and RNA stability. By offering a cost-effective, scalable solution for precise RNA profiling in rare biological samples, this breakthrough tool unlocks new avenues for exploring cccDNA biology and accelerating antiviral drug development.

Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus infection persists as a global health crisis, driving life-threatening liver pathologies such as hepatocellular carcinoma.
Design and population
By offering a cost-effective, scalable solution for precise RNA profiling in rare biological samples, this breakthrough tool unlocks new avenues for exploring cccDNA biology and accelerating antiviral drug development.
Core methods
To address this bottleneck, we developed a multiplexed assay named ddPCR for Overlapping Transcripts (ddOTs) capable of simultaneously quantifying all major HBV RNA species, including splice variants, with unprecedented specificity and sensitivity.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
This method overcomes current limitations by leveraging the high-resolution power of ddPCR to deconvolute overlapping transcripts, enabling direct interrogation of individual promoter/enhancer activities and RNA stability.
Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific scientific or public-health implication.

lncRNA ZNF667-AS1通过调节miR-210-3p上调CPEB2抑制乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌

lncRNA ZNF667-AS1 suppresses hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma by regulating miR-210-3p to upregulate CPEB2
作者： Huimin Cong, Guobing Zhu, Yingzhou Xu, Linlin Wu, Hongfang Gu, Chunyan Dai
摘要中文翻译探讨长链非编码RNA(lncRNA)ZNF667-AS1在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌(HCC)中的生物学功能、临床意义和调控机制。本研究纳入了 107 名 HBV 相关 HCC 患者、105 名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者和 105 名健康对照者。采用实时定量聚合酶链反应(RT-qPCR)检测ZNF667-AS1、miR-210-3p、CPEB2的表达水平。分析ZNF667-AS1表达与患者临床特征的关联,并进行受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析以评估其诊断价值。进行生物信息学预测和双荧光素酶报告基因测定,以验证 ZNF667-AS1 和 miR-210-3p 之间以及 miR-210-3p 和 CPEB2 之间的靶向关联。应用细胞计数试剂盒-8 (CCK-8) 和 Transwell 检测来评估细胞转染后 HCC 细胞的生物学功能。在 HBV 相关 HCC 患者的组织样本和 HBV 相关 HCC 细胞系中,ZNF667-AS1 和 CPEB2 的表达水平显着下调。ROC曲线分析显示,ZNF667-AS1诊断CHB与HBV相关HCC的曲线下面积(AUC)达到0.841。miR-210-3p被验证为ZNF667-AS1的直接靶标,并且观察到它们的表达水平之间存在显着的负相关。ZNF667-AS1 的过表达抑制 HCC 细胞的增殖、迁移和侵袭,而这种抑制作用可通过转染 miR-210-3p 模拟物而逆转。LncRNA ZNF667-AS1 在 HBV 相关 HCC 中下调,并通过靶向 miR-210-3p 上调 CPEB2 表达发挥体外肿瘤抑制作用,从而为 HBV 相关 HCC 诊断 提供潜在的辅助生物标志物,并为理解疾病 诊断 提供新的调控轴。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在阐明长链非编码RNA(lncRNA)ZNF667-AS1在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌(HCC)中的生物学功能、临床相关性和调控机制。
设计与对象
该研究招募了 107 名 HBV 相关 HCC 患者、105 名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者和 105 名健康对照,以研究 lncRNA ZNF667-AS1 表达及其临床关联。
核心方法
RT-qPCR 用于量化 ZNF667-AS1、miR-210-3p 和 CPEB2 表达水平。生物信息学预测和双荧光素酶报告基因测定验证了 ZNF667-AS1 和 miR-210-3p 之间以及 miR-210-3p 和 CPEB2 之间的靶向相互作用。CCK-8 和 Transwell 检测评估转染后 HCC 细胞的增殖、迁移和侵袭。
主要结果
ZNF667-AS1和CPEB2在HBV相关HCC组织和HBV相关HCC细胞系中显着下调。ROC 曲线分析显示,ZNF667-AS1 区分 HBV 相关 HCC 和 CHB 的 AUC 为 0.841。
解释与创新
作者解释说,lncRNA ZNF667-AS1 通过海绵 miR-210-3p 上调 CPEB2,在 HBV 相关 HCC 中发挥肿瘤抑制作用,为疾病 发病机制 提供潜在的辅助诊断生物标志物和新的调控轴。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,ZNF667-AS1 可以作为 HBV 相关 HCC 的诊断生物标志物,并突出 ZNF667-AS1/miR-210-3p/CPEB2 轴内的潜在治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖体外测定和单中心 队列研究 而受到限制,这限制了普遍性和因果推理;证据强度被评估为中等。

lncRNA ZNF667-AS1 suppresses hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma by regulating miR-210-3p to upregulate CPEB2

Authors: Huimin Cong, Guobing Zhu, Yingzhou Xu, Linlin Wu, Hongfang Gu, Chunyan Dai
Original abstract (English)
To explore the biological functions, clinical significance, and regulatory mechanism of long non-coding RNA (lncRNA) ZNF667-AS1 in hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). One hundred and seven patients with HBV-related HCC, one hundred and five patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and one hundred and five healthy controls were enrolled in this study. Real-time quantitative polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR) was used to detect the expression levels of ZNF667-AS1, miR-210-3p, CPEB2. The association between ZNF667-AS1 expression and clinical characteristics of patients were analyzed, and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis was performed to evaluate its diagnostic value. Bioinformatics prediction and dual-luciferase reporter assays were conducted to verify the targeted associations between ZNF667-AS1 and miR-210-3p, as well as between miR-210-3p and CPEB2. Cell Counting Kit-8 (CCK-8) and Transwell assays were applied to assess the biological functions of HCC cells after cell transfection. The expression levels of ZNF667-AS1 and CPEB2 were significantly downregulated in tissue samples from patients with HBV-related HCC and in HBV-associated HCC cell lines. ROC curve analysis showed the area under the curve (AUC) of ZNF667-AS1 for diagnosing HBV-related HCC from CHB reached 0.841. miR-210-3p was validated as a direct target of ZNF667-AS1, and a significant negative correlation was observed between their expression levels. Overexpression of ZNF667-AS1 inhibited the proliferation, migration, and invasion of HCC cells, whereas this inhibitory effect was reversed by transfection with miR-210-3p mimic. LncRNA ZNF667-AS1 is downregulated in HBV-related HCC and exerts in vitro tumor‐suppressive effects by targeting miR-210-3p to upregulate CPEB2 expression, thus providing a potential auxiliary biomarker for HBV-related HCC diagnosis and a novel regulatory axis for understanding disease pathogenesis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to elucidate the biological functions, clinical relevance, and regulatory mechanism of long non-coding RNA (lncRNA) ZNF667-AS1 in hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Design and population
The study enrolled 107 patients with HBV-related HCC, 105 patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), and 105 healthy controls to investigate lncRNA ZNF667-AS1 expression and its clinical associations.
Core methods
RT-qPCR was used to quantify ZNF667-AS1, miR-210-3p, and CPEB2 expression levels. Bioinformatics prediction and dual-luciferase reporter assays validated the targeted interactions between ZNF667-AS1 and miR-210-3p, and between miR-210-3p and CPEB2. CCK-8 and Transwell assays assessed HCC cell proliferation, migration, and invasion after transfection.
Main results
ZNF667-AS1 and CPEB2 were significantly downregulated in HBV-related HCC tissues and HBV-associated HCC cell lines. ROC curve analysis showed an AUC of 0.841 for ZNF667-AS1 in distinguishing HBV-related HCC from CHB.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that lncRNA ZNF667-AS1 acts as a tumor suppressor in HBV-related HCC by sponging miR-210-3p to upregulate CPEB2, providing a potential auxiliary diagnostic biomarker and a novel regulatory axis for disease pathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that ZNF667-AS1 could serve as a diagnostic biomarker for HBV-related HCC and highlight a potential therapeutic target within the ZNF667-AS1/miR-210-3p/CPEB2 axis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro assays and a single-center cohort, which constrains generalizability and causal inference; the evidence strength is assessed as moderate.

慢性乙型肝炎 肝脏生化和止血标志物评估肝硬化严重程度的诊断性能

Diagnostic performance of liver biochemical and hemostatic markers for assessing cirrhosis severity in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Chunhua Luo, Yucheng Luo, Qianyuan Li, Xiaolin Zhou, Jun Luo
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在探讨肝脏生化和止血(包括凝血、抗凝和纤溶)标志物在评估 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者肝硬化严重程度方面的效用。在本研究中,共有 28 名健康对照者和 78 名患有 CHB 相关肝硬化的患者入组。测量常规实验室指标,包括血小板计数(PLT)、白蛋白(ALB)、总胆红素(TBIL)、肌酐(CREA)、凝血酶原时间(PT)、活化部分凝血活酶时间(APTT)、因子VIII(FVIII)、凝血酶原活性(PA)、蛋白S(PS)、蛋白C(PC)、抗凝血酶III(AT-III)和D-二聚体(D-D)。还探讨了测量参数与 Child-Pugh 评分 (CPS)/终末期肝病模型评分 (MELD) 之间的相关性。进行受试者工作特征 (ROC) 曲线分析,以评估这些指标区分轻度和中度至重度 CHB 相关肝硬化的能力。与健康对照相比,CHB 相关肝硬化患者的 ALB、PA、PS、PC、AT-III 和 PLT 水平显着降低,而 TBIL、PT、APTT、FVIII 和 D-D 水平显着升高 (p < 0.05)。ALB、PA、AT-III、PS 和 PC 与 CPS 和 MELD 均呈显着负相关 (p < 0.01)。TBIL、PT 和 APTT 表现出显着的正相关性 (p < 0.01)。ROC曲线分析显示,AT-III区分肝硬化严重程度的AUC为0.95(95% CI:0.88-1.00),其次是PC(AUC:0.87)和PA(AUC:0.87)。正如预期的那样,Child-Pugh 成分(ALB、TBIL、PT)与分数显示出很强的关联性。在未纳入 CPS 的标志物中,AT-III 与 CHB 相关肝硬化的严重程度显着相关,并且在 CHB 相关肝硬化分期方面表现出有希望的区分能力。它可能支持动态监测 HBV 相关肝硬化进展的低成本、客观框架。尽管 ALB、TBIL 和 PT 也与疾病严重程度相关,但这些关联会受到合并偏倚的影响,因此代表的是证实性而非新颖的发现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 会导致肝硬化,但用于评估其严重程度的非侵入性标记物有限。这项研究询问肝脏生化和止血标志物是否可以客观地对慢性乙型肝炎相关的肝硬化进行分期。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究招募了 28 名健康对照者和 78 名患有 CHB 相关肝硬化的患者来评估诊断标志物。
核心方法
测量常规实验室指标,包括血小板计数、白蛋白、总胆红素、肌酐、凝血酶原时间、活化部分凝血活酶时间、因子VIII、凝血酶原活性、蛋白S、蛋白C、抗凝血酶III和D-二聚体。探讨了与 Child-Pugh 和 MELD 评分的相关性,并通过 ROC 曲线分析评估了轻度和中度至重度肝硬化之间的区别。
主要结果
Patients with CHB-related cirrhosis had significantly lower albumin, prothrombin activity, protein S, protein C, antithrombin III and platelet count, and significantly higher total bilirubin, prothrombin time, activated partial thromboplastin time, factor VIII and D-dimer than healthy controls (p < 0.05). Antithrombin III showed the highest area under the ROC curve (0.95, 95% CI 0.88–1.00) for distinguishing cirrhosis severity, followed by protein C and prothrombin activity (each AUC 0.87).
解释与创新
抗凝血酶 III 与 CHB 相关的肝硬化严重程度显着相关,并且对分期表现出很强的区分能力,而白蛋白、总胆红素和凝血酶原时间的关联反映了掺入偏差,并且是证实性的而非新颖的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,抗凝血酶 III 可以在临床实践中低成本、客观地监测 HBV 相关的肝硬化进展。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计和小样本限制了因果推断和普遍性;由于潜在的选择偏差和未经调整的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Diagnostic performance of liver biochemical and hemostatic markers for assessing cirrhosis severity in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Chunhua Luo, Yucheng Luo, Qianyuan Li, Xiaolin Zhou, Jun Luo
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to explore the utility of liver biochemical and hemostatic (including coagulation, anticoagulation, and fibrinolysis) markers for assessing cirrhosis severity in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients. In this current study, a total of 28 healthy controls and 78 patients with CHB-related cirrhosis were enrolled. Routine laboratory markers including platelet count (PLT), albumin (ALB), total bilirubin (TBIL), creatinine (CREA), prothrombin time (PT), activated partial thromboplastin time (APTT), factor VIII (FVIII), prothrombin activity (PA), protein S (PS), protein C (PC), antithrombin III (AT-III) and D-dimer (D-D) were measured. Correlations between the measured parameters and Child-Pugh score (CPS)/ Model for End-Stage Liver Disease score (MELD) were also explored. Receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis was performed to evaluate the ability of these indicators to discriminate between mild and moderate-to-severe CHB-related cirrhosis. Patients with CHB-related cirrhosis exhibited significantly lower levels of ALB, PA, PS, PC, AT-III, and PLT, and significantly higher levels of TBIL, PT, APTT, FVIII, and D-D, compared with healthy controls ( p < 0.05). ALB, PA, AT-III, PS, and PC showed significant negative correlations with both CPS and MELD ( p < 0.01). TBIL, PT, and APTT demonstrated significant positive correlations ( p < 0.01). ROC curve analysis revealed that AT-III achieved an AUC of 0.95 (95% CI: 0.88–1.00) for distinguishing cirrhosis severity, followed by PC (AUC: 0.87) and PA (AUC: 0.87). As expected, Child-Pugh components (ALB, TBIL, PT) showed strong associations with the score. Among markers not incorporated into the CPS, AT-III is significantly associated with the severity of CHB-related cirrhosis and exhibits promising discriminatory ability for staging CHB-related cirrhosis. It may support a low-cost, objective framework for dynamic monitoring of HBV-related cirrhosis progression. Although ALB, TBIL, and PT were also correlated with disease severity, these associations are subject to incorporation bias and thus represent confirmatory rather than novel findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) leads to cirrhosis, but noninvasive markers for assessing its severity are limited. This study asked whether liver biochemical and hemostatic markers can objectively stage CHB-related cirrhosis.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study enrolled 28 healthy controls and 78 patients with CHB-related cirrhosis to evaluate diagnostic markers.
Core methods
Routine laboratory markers including platelet count, albumin, total bilirubin, creatinine, prothrombin time, activated partial thromboplastin time, factor VIII, prothrombin activity, protein S, protein C, antithrombin III and D-dimer were measured. Correlations with Child-Pugh and MELD scores were explored, and ROC curve analysis assessed discrimination between mild and moderate-to-severe cirrhosis.
Main results
Patients with CHB-related cirrhosis had significantly lower albumin, prothrombin activity, protein S, protein C, antithrombin III and platelet count, and significantly higher total bilirubin, prothrombin time, activated partial thromboplastin time, factor VIII and D-dimer than healthy controls (p < 0.05). Antithrombin III showed the highest area under the ROC curve (0.95, 95% CI 0.88–1.00) for distinguishing cirrhosis severity, followed by protein C and prothrombin activity (each AUC 0.87).
Interpretation and novelty
Antithrombin III was significantly associated with CHB-related cirrhosis severity and showed strong discriminatory ability for staging, whereas albumin, total bilirubin and prothrombin time associations reflected incorporation bias and were confirmatory rather than novel.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest antithrombin III could enable low-cost, objective monitoring of HBV-related cirrhosis progression in clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and small sample limit causal inference and generalisability; evidence strength is moderate due to potential selection bias and unadjusted confounding.

CXCL16基因启动子甲基化与慢性乙型肝炎患者的炎症相关

CXCL16 gene promoter methylation is associated with inflammation in patients with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Zhezhe Tian, Miaomiao Xu, Hongfei Li, Yunxin Yao, Yuchen Fan, Shuai Gao, Kai Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染引起持续性肝脏炎症,趋化因子 CXCL16 是肝损伤的关键介质,但其在 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中的表观遗传调控仍不清楚。该研究纳入了 217 名慢性乙型肝炎患者(113 名 HBeAg 阳性,104 名 HBeAg 阴性)和 27 名健康对照,以研究 CXCL16 启动子甲基化、mRNA 和蛋白表达,以及它们与炎症指标和肝损伤标志物的相关性。结果显示,CHB 患者中,尤其是 HBeAg 阳性病例中,CXCL16 启动子甲基化显着降低,而 CXCL16 mRNA 和血清水平显着升高。CXCL16启动子甲基化与CXCL16表达、HBeAg状态、ALT、AST、TNF-α和IL-1β水平呈负相关。ROC分析表明CXCL16启动子甲基化对于评估CHB患者的肝脏炎症损伤具有良好的诊断价值。总之,CXCL16启动子低甲基化与CHB患者CXCL16表达升高和肝脏炎症加剧密切相关,可能作为评估CHB相关肝脏炎症的潜在非侵入性生物标志物。这些发现丰富了HBV相关肝脏炎症的表观遗传机制,并为临床评估和干预提供了新的靶点CXCL16。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨CXCL16在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)中的表观遗传调控及其与炎症的关系,因为HBV感染会引起持续性肝脏炎症,而CXCL16是肝损伤的关键介质,但其表观遗传调控仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究招募了 217 名慢性乙型肝炎患者(113 名 HBeAg 阳性,104 名 HBeAg 阴性)和 27 名健康对照者,以检查 CXCL16 启动子甲基化、mRNA 和蛋白表达,及其与炎症指标和肝损伤标志物的相关性。
核心方法
该研究使用甲基化特异性 PCR 测量 CXCL16 启动子甲基化,通过定量实时 PCR 评估 mRNA 表达,并通过 ELISA 评估血清 CXCL16 水平。使用统计方法分析相关性,并采用 ROC 分析来评估诊断价值。
主要结果
在 CHB 患者中,尤其是 HBeAg 阳性病例中,CXCL16 启动子甲基化显着较低,而 CXCL16 mRNA 和血清水平显着较高。CXCL16启动子甲基化与CXCL16表达、HBeAg状态、ALT、AST、TNF-α和IL-1β水平呈负相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CXCL16启动子低甲基化与慢性乙型肝炎患者CXCL16表达升高和肝脏炎症加剧密切相关,为乙型肝炎相关炎症的表观遗传机制提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为开发用于评估 CHB 相关肝脏炎症的非侵入性生物标志物提供信息,并为治疗 HBV 引起的肝损伤的潜在治疗靶点提供见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于仅进行摘要分析,虽然结果与报告的关联一致,但缺乏详细的统计方法和结果解释使证据的强度降低到中等。

CXCL16 gene promoter methylation is associated with inflammation in patients with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Zhezhe Tian, Miaomiao Xu, Hongfei Li, Yunxin Yao, Yuchen Fan, Shuai Gao, Kai Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection causes persistent hepatic inflammation, and chemokine CXCL16 serves as a key mediator in liver injury, but its epigenetic regulation in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains unclear. This study enrolled 217 CHB patients (113 HBeAg‐positive, 104 HBeAg‐negative) and 27 healthy controls to investigate CXCL16 promoter methylation, mRNA and protein expression, as well as their correlations with inflammatory indices and liver injury markers. The results showed that CXCL16 promoter methylation was significantly lower, while CXCL16 mRNA and serum levels were markedly higher in CHB patients, especially in HBeAg‐positive cases. CXCL16 promoter methylation was negatively correlated with CXCL16 expression, HBeAg status, ALT, AST, TNF‐α and IL‐1βlevels. ROC analysis indicated that CXCL16 promoter methylation had favorable diagnostic value for evaluating hepatic inflammatory injury in CHB patients. In conclusion, CXCL16 promoter hypomethylation is closely associated with elevated CXCL16 expression and exacerbated hepatic inflammation in CHB patients, and may serve as a potential non‐invasive biomarker for assessing CHB‐related liver inflammation. These findings enrich the epigenetic mechanism underlying HBV‐related hepatic inflammation and provide a novel target which is CXCL16 for clinical evaluation and intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the epigenetic regulation of CXCL16 in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and its association with inflammation, as HBV infection causes persistent hepatic inflammation and CXCL16 is a key mediator in liver injury, yet its epigenetic regulation remains unclear.
Design and population
The study enrolled 217 CHB patients (113 HBeAg‐positive, 104 HBeAg‐negative) and 27 healthy controls to examine CXCL16 promoter methylation, mRNA and protein expression, and their correlations with inflammatory indices and liver injury markers.
Core methods
The study measured CXCL16 promoter methylation using methylation-specific PCR, assessed mRNA expression via quantitative real-time PCR, and evaluated serum CXCL16 levels through ELISA. Correlations were analyzed using statistical methods, and ROC analysis was employed to assess diagnostic value.
Main results
CXCL16 promoter methylation was significantly lower, while CXCL16 mRNA and serum levels were markedly higher in CHB patients, particularly in HBeAg‐positive cases. CXCL16 promoter methylation was negatively correlated with CXCL16 expression, HBeAg status, ALT, AST, TNF‐α and IL‐1β levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CXCL16 promoter hypomethylation is closely associated with elevated CXCL16 expression and exacerbated hepatic inflammation in CHB patients, offering a novel perspective on the epigenetic mechanisms of HBV-related inflammation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of non-invasive biomarkers for assessing CHB-related liver inflammation and provide insights into potential therapeutic targets for managing HBV-induced hepatic injury.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and while the results are consistent with the reported associations, the lack of detailed statistical methods and outcome interpretations reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

治疗-Naïve 慢性乙型肝炎 中八种非侵入性纤维化评分的基于活检的头对头比较

Biopsy-Based Head-to-Head Comparison of Eight Non-Invasive Fibrosis Scores in Treatment-Naïve Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Seda Zor, Dilek Yılmaz, Hilal Ekici
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目的 准确的纤维化分期对于治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 至关重要。尽管已提出几种非侵入性纤维化评分作为肝活检的替代方法,但它们在纤维化阶段的比较表现仍不确定。这项研究比较了八种常用的非侵入性纤维化评分,用于检测 治疗 初治慢性乙型肝炎患者的显着、晚期和严重纤维化。材料和方法 这项回顾性研究纳入了 2017 年 5 月至 2024 年 5 月期间接受肝活检的 治疗 初治慢性乙型肝炎患者。使用 Ishak 评分系统对纤维化进行分期。使用接受者操作特征 (ROC) 分析评估 APRI、FIB-4、KING、GUCI、AAR、API、INPR 和 GPR 检测 F ≥ 2、F ≥ 3 和 F ≥ 4 纤维化的情况。使用约登指数确定最佳截止值。计算AUROC、敏感性、特异性、阳性预测值和阴性预测值。使用 DeLong 检验进行成对 AUROC 比较。结果共纳入268例患者。所有评分在检测显着纤维化(F ≥ 2)方面表现不佳,AUROC 值低于 0.60。晚期和严重纤维化的诊断准确性得到提高。KING 在晚期纤维化方面取得了最高的 AUROC (0.807),其次是 FIB-4 (0.797),而 GPR 在严重纤维化方面取得了最高的 AUROC (0.929)。德隆分析表明,表现最好的分数之间没有显着差异。结论 评估的评分均未表现出足够的准确性来替代肝活检来检测显着的纤维化。然而,FIB-4、KING 和 GPR 在晚期和严重纤维化方面表现出良好的性能,可能有助于初步风险分层和识别需要肝活检进一步评估的患者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确的纤维化分期对于治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 至关重要,尽管几种非侵入性纤维化评分在不同纤维化阶段的比较表现仍不确定。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 2017 年 5 月至 2024 年 5 月期间接受过肝活检的 治疗 初治慢性乙型肝炎患者。
核心方法
使用 Ishak 评分系统对纤维化进行分期,并使用 ROC 分析评估八种非侵入性纤维化评分(APRI、FIB-4、KING、GUCI、AAR、API、INPR、GPR)以检测 F ≥ 2、F ≥ 3 和 F ≥ 4 纤维化,最佳截止值由 Youden 指数确定。
主要结果
总共纳入268名患者;所有评分在检测显着纤维化 (F ≥ 2) 方面表现不佳,AUROC 值低于 0.60,但晚期和严重纤维化的诊断准确性有所提高,KING 在晚期纤维化方面取得了最高的 AUROC (0.807),在严重纤维化方面取得了最高的 GPR (0.929)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,尽管 FIB-4、KING 和 GPR 在检测晚期和严重纤维化方面表现出良好的性能,但所有评估的评分都没有表现出足够的准确性来替代肝活检来检测显着的纤维化。
科研与公卫意义
FIB-4、KING 和 GPR 可能有助于初步风险分层和识别需要肝活检进一步评估的患者,从而可能减少临床实践中不必要的活检。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,仅限于 治疗 初治患者,由于单中心设计和依赖活检作为参考标准,证据强度评估为中等。

Biopsy-Based Head-to-Head Comparison of Eight Non-Invasive Fibrosis Scores in Treatment-Naïve Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Seda Zor, Dilek Yılmaz, Hilal Ekici
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background and Aims Accurate fibrosis staging is essential for managing chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Although several non-invasive fibrosis scores have been proposed as alternatives to liver biopsy, their comparative performance across fibrosis stages remains uncertain. This study compared eight commonly used non-invasive fibrosis scores for detecting significant, advanced, and severe fibrosis in treatment-naïve patients with CHB. Materials and Methods This retrospective study included treatment-naïve patients with CHB who underwent liver biopsy between May 2017 and May 2024. Fibrosis was staged using the Ishak scoring system. APRI, FIB-4, KING, GUCI, AAR, API, INPR, and GPR were evaluated for detecting F ≥ 2, F ≥ 3, and F ≥ 4 fibrosis using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) analysis. Optimal cut-off values were determined using the Youden index. AUROC, sensitivity, specificity, positive predictive value, and negative predictive value were calculated. Pairwise AUROC comparisons were performed using DeLong's test. Results A total of 268 patients were included. All scores showed poor performance for detecting significant fibrosis (F ≥ 2), with AUROC values below 0.60. Diagnostic accuracy improved for advanced and severe fibrosis. KING achieved the highest AUROC for advanced fibrosis (0.807), followed by FIB-4 (0.797), whereas GPR showed the highest AUROC for severe fibrosis (0.929). DeLong analysis demonstrated no significant differences among the best-performing scores. Conclusion None of the evaluated scores demonstrated sufficient accuracy to replace liver biopsy for detecting significant fibrosis. However, FIB-4, KING, and GPR showed good performance for advanced and severe fibrosis and may be useful for initial risk stratification and identifying patients who require further evaluation with liver biopsy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate fibrosis staging is essential for managing chronic hepatitis B (CHB), although the comparative performance of several non-invasive fibrosis scores across fibrosis stages remains uncertain.
Design and population
This retrospective study included treatment-naïve patients with CHB who underwent liver biopsy between May 2017 and May 2024.
Core methods
Fibrosis was staged using the Ishak scoring system, and eight non-invasive fibrosis scores (APRI, FIB-4, KING, GUCI, AAR, API, INPR, GPR) were evaluated for detecting F ≥ 2, F ≥ 3, and F ≥ 4 fibrosis using ROC analysis with optimal cut-off values determined by the Youden index.
Main results
A total of 268 patients were included; all scores showed poor performance for detecting significant fibrosis (F ≥ 2) with AUROC values below 0.60, but diagnostic accuracy improved for advanced and severe fibrosis, with KING achieving the highest AUROC for advanced fibrosis (0.807) and GPR for severe fibrosis (0.929).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that none of the evaluated scores demonstrated sufficient accuracy to replace liver biopsy for detecting significant fibrosis, though FIB-4, KING, and GPR showed good performance for advanced and severe fibrosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
FIB-4, KING, and GPR may be useful for initial risk stratification and identifying patients who require further evaluation with liver biopsy, potentially reducing unnecessary biopsies in clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was retrospective and limited to treatment-naïve patients, with evidence strength assessed as moderate due to the single-center design and reliance on biopsy as the reference standard.

越南多个医疗保健部门乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎检测的诊断能力和利用率

Diagnostic capacity and utilization of Hepatitis B and C testing across multiple health care sectors in Vietnam
作者： Quan Le, Ngoc Thai Truong, Doan Y. Dao, Amy Trang, Van Nguyen, Hang Dao, Bao Q. Duong, Hai Phan, Mary Smith, Chase Yonamine
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 WHO 的目标是到 2030 年消除 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 作为公共卫生威胁,但 诊断 在许多低收入和中等收入国家仍然不理想。人们经常强调获得诊断的机会,但现有能力的使用程度却很少被描述。我们量化了三个与初级保健相关的越南设施的 HBV/HCV 检测的装机容量和利用率,并描述了疾病阶段。方法 我们在 2018 年至 2020 年期间对私人诊断网络、公立地区医院和教学/研究机构的血清学(HBsAg、抗 HCV)和分子(HBV DNA、HCV RNA)检测进行了横断面库存和使用审核。利用率是进行的测试除以最大理论容量。诊断 有关纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的设施级数据来自常规记录。结果 2019 年(COVID-19 之前的参考年),私人网络的血清学和 PCR 利用率分别为 50.0% 和 18.8%,公立医院为 5.0% 和 21.3%,教学机构最低(3.5% 和 3.0%)。2018 年至 2020 年期间,乙肝病毒中的晚期纤维化(≥ F3)从 11.4% 上升至 16.6%,丙肝病毒中的晚期纤维化从 21.6% 上升至 37.5%。大多数 HCC 发生在 HBV 感染个体中(两个较大站点分别为 52.3% 和 21.4%),并且与大量未使用容量共存;我们没有评估测试的准确性。结论 越南拥有大量的 HBV/HCV 诊断基础设施,但使用不均衡。由于数据是按机构汇总的,因此这些是关联,而不是患者层面的因果关系。优化现有平台的使用可能有助于解决系统效率低下的问题并支持消除工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
世界卫生组织的目标是到 2030 年消除 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 作为公共卫生威胁,但 诊断 在许多低收入和中等收入国家仍然不理想。人们经常强调获得诊断的机会,但现有能力的使用程度却很少被描述。
设计与对象
我们在 2018 年至 2020 年期间对越南的一家私人诊断网络、一家公立地区医院和一家教学/研究机构进行了血清学(HBsAg、抗 HCV)和分子(HBV DNA、HCV RNA)检测的横断面库存和使用审核。
核心方法
利用率的计算方法是进行的测试除以最大理论容量,并从常规记录中提取 诊断 的纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 设施级数据。
主要结果
2019 年(COVID-19 之前的参考年),私人网络的血清学和 PCR 利用率分别为 50.0% 和 18.8%,公立医院为 5.0% 和 21.3%,教学机构最低(3.5% 和 3.0%)。2018 年至 2020 年期间,乙肝病毒中的晚期纤维化(≥ F3)从 11.4% 上升至 16.6%,丙肝病毒中的晚期纤维化从 21.6% 上升至 37.5%。
解释与创新
越南拥有大量的 HBV/HCV 诊断基础设施,但使用不均衡。大多数 HCC 发生在 HBV 感染个体中(两个较大地点分别为 52.3% 和 21.4%),并且与大量未使用容量并存。
科研与公卫意义
优化现有平台的使用可能有助于解决系统效率低下的问题并支持消除工作。研究结果强调需要提高诊断能力的利用率以减少晚期肝病。
局限与证据强度
由于数据是按机构汇总的,因此这些是关联,而不是患者层面的因果关系。由于横截面设计和缺乏测试准确性评估,证据强度中等。

Diagnostic capacity and utilization of Hepatitis B and C testing across multiple health care sectors in Vietnam

Authors: Quan Le, Ngoc Thai Truong, Doan Y. Dao, Amy Trang, Van Nguyen, Hang Dao, Bao Q. Duong, Hai Phan, Mary Smith, Chase Yonamine
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The WHO aims to eliminate hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) as public health threats by 2030, yet diagnosis remains suboptimal in many low- and middle-income countries. Access to diagnostics is often emphasized, but how far existing capacity is used is poorly characterized. We quantified installed capacity and utilization of HBV/HCV testing across three primary-care–linked Vietnamese facilities and described disease stage. Methods We conducted a cross-sectional inventory and utilization audit of serologic (HBsAg, anti-HCV) and molecular (HBV DNA, HCV RNA) testing during 2018–2020 at a private diagnostic network, a public district hospital, and a teaching/research institute. Utilization was tests performed divided by maximum theoretical capacity. Facility-level data on fibrosis, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) at diagnosis were drawn from routine records. Results In 2019 (pre-COVID-19 reference year), serologic and PCR utilization were 50.0% and 18.8% at the private network, 5.0% and 21.3% at the public hospital, and lowest at the teaching institute (3.5% and 3.0%). Advanced fibrosis (≥ F3) rose over 2018–2020 from 11.4% to 16.6% in HBV and 21.6% to 37.5% in HCV. Most HCC occurred in individuals with HBV infection (52.3% and 21.4% at the two larger sites) and coexisted with high unused capacity; we did not assess testing accuracy. Conclusions Substantial HBV/HCV diagnostic infrastructure exists in Vietnam but is unevenly used. Because data were facility-aggregated, these are associations, not patient-level causal relationships. Optimizing use of existing platforms may help address systemic inefficiencies and support elimination efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The WHO aims to eliminate hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) as public health threats by 2030, yet diagnosis remains suboptimal in many low- and middle-income countries. Access to diagnostics is often emphasized, but how far existing capacity is used is poorly characterized.
Design and population
We conducted a cross-sectional inventory and utilization audit of serologic (HBsAg, anti-HCV) and molecular (HBV DNA, HCV RNA) testing during 2018–2020 at a private diagnostic network, a public district hospital, and a teaching/research institute in Vietnam.
Core methods
Utilization was calculated as tests performed divided by maximum theoretical capacity, and facility-level data on fibrosis, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) at diagnosis were drawn from routine records.
Main results
In 2019 (pre-COVID-19 reference year), serologic and PCR utilization were 50.0% and 18.8% at the private network, 5.0% and 21.3% at the public hospital, and lowest at the teaching institute (3.5% and 3.0%). Advanced fibrosis (≥ F3) rose over 2018–2020 from 11.4% to 16.6% in HBV and 21.6% to 37.5% in HCV.
Interpretation and novelty
Substantial HBV/HCV diagnostic infrastructure exists in Vietnam but is unevenly used. Most HCC occurred in individuals with HBV infection (52.3% and 21.4% at the two larger sites) and coexisted with high unused capacity.
Scientific and public-health significance
Optimizing use of existing platforms may help address systemic inefficiencies and support elimination efforts. The findings highlight the need to improve utilization of diagnostic capacity to reduce advanced liver disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
Because data were facility-aggregated, these are associations, not patient-level causal relationships. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and lack of testing accuracy assessment.
